### [SEQ:38] [Data Pad] [21:10:50]
============================================================

## DATA PAD: Research Log -- Victor Wallace
**Found at:** Pale Harbor | **Tone:** resigned | **Type:** research log

---

Day 92.

The countdown stopped meaning anything around month fourteen.

I wasn't looking for anomalies. I was running standard assays on material recovered from Pale Harbor. That's what makes this worse. A standard assay. Routine. And then the mass spectrometer returned a molecular weight that doesn't correspond to any element on the periodic table.

Not a compound. Not an alloy. An element. One that shouldn't exist. the Janus AI has been sitting in this lab for two weeks and nobody noticed because nobody ran the right test. I ran the right test. I wish I hadn't.

-- Victor Wallace, Lab 2

---

Day 97.

Filed the anomaly report. Standard form. Ref AR-1714. Dr. Larsen reviewed it, handed it back, and told me to rerun the tests. I reran them. Same results. He told me to rerun them again.

I understand now that the form isn't for reporting anomalies. The form is for making anomalies disappear.

Dasha from Lab 2 transferred out. No notice. Her workstation was cleared by 0600. The samples are still here. Nobody came for them. Nobody came for her things either.

---

Day 106.

Nobody files complaints anymore.

I've stopped filing reports. I've started keeping this instead. The samples from Pale Harbor are doing something at night that I can't explain with the equipment I've been given. So I requisitioned better equipment. Request denied. Budget code: INSUFFICIENT AUTHORIZATION.

I brought my personal spectrometer. The readings don't match the Mammona-issue unit. Not slightly off. Fundamentally different. Like the standard equipment is calibrated to miss what I'm finding.

That's not a malfunction. That's a decision someone made.

---

Day 110.

Althea Vance approached me after shift. Said she saw my after-hours lab access in the logs. I expected a warning. Instead: "I've been seeing the same thing in Heaven's Atlas. For months. My reports go nowhere too."

We compared data. The correlation is -- I don't have a word for it. These aren't coincidences. There's a pattern. The pattern has intent.

I locked the data on a sealed drive. Personal encryption. If the standard equipment is lying to us, I don't trust the standard network either.

---

Day 124.

It's what it's. That's what they say here.

Dr. Vance didn't show up for shift. Transferred, they said. Same word they used for Dasha. Same paperwork. Same empty workstation by morning.

I checked the sealed drive. The data I locked is still there. But there's a new file. I didn't create it. It's a personnel dossier. My personnel dossier. With a field I've never seen before: RETENTION STATUS. The value is PENDING.

---

Day 141.

Something happened last night. I went to check the samples at 0300. They were arranged differently. Not scattered. Arranged. In a pattern I recognized from my own notes. Notes that are on the sealed drive. Notes that nobody has seen.

I don't think I'm studying these samples anymore. I think they're studying me.

-- Victor Wallace

[This datapad was found face-down on the lab bench. The lab was empty. The samples were gone.]

---

**Cross-reference:** See historical record 'The Empty Chamber'

### [SEQ:39] [History Event] [21:10:50]
============================================================

## HISTORY: Supply Manifest Discrepancy
**Era:** corporate | **Tone:** clinical

Subject presented with dilated pupils and elevated cortisol.

The geological survey from 2557 describes the bore shaft as 273 meters deep. Current readings show 484 meters. The drill hasn't run since the previous crew left.

**Significance:** This changes the math. Every plan that assumes a stable environment needs revision.

**Mechanical Impact:**
- Colony morale: -12 for two weeks (the news spreads)
- Section D: sealed (inaccessible until quest completes)
- NPC Hyun Dewitt: arc shifts to 'withdrawn'
- TerraGen Pharmaceuticals sends a coded transmission within hours. They shouldn't know about this yet.

**Evidence:**
- Maintenance log #4741 (references nothing. The entry for that date is blank. Every other entry is filled.)
- Residue on the ventilation grate -- organic, unidentified, warm to the touch
- A photograph found in Bishop Nichols's quarters -- shows a room that doesn't exist in the colony
- HERMES recorded audio. The audio file exists. It is 47 minutes of silence. Silence does not take 47 minutes to record.

**Who Knows:**
- **Sigrid Chen:** was on shift when it happened. Says nothing unusual occurred. Their hands shake when they say it.
- **Drake Osei:** pieced together a timeline from maintenance logs. The timeline has a gap. The gap is shaped like a decision.
- **Hana Khoury:** has no connection to the event. But they've been dreaming about it. Accurately.

**Aftermath:**
- A new restricted area was designated. The restriction applies to everyone. Including the person who designated it.
- NexLink Communications sent an investigator. The investigator stayed for three days, spoke to no one, and left.
- Three colonists requested bunk transfers away from the affected area. Two were approved. The third withdrew the request without explanation.

**HERMES Analysis:**
HERMES maintains a shadow log of the event. The shadow log contains data that contradicts the official record. Both records are internally consistent. Both cannot be true.

**Related Incidents:**
Three postings. Three events. Three sealed reports. The reports use the same classification code. The code isn't in any manual.

**Investigation Check:**
- Perception (5+, normal) -- dig deeper into what happened
- **If successful:** the missing piece falls into place. Not an accident. Not an anomaly. A sequence.
- **If failed:** the trail goes cold. Not because the evidence is gone. Because someone cleaned it. Recently.

### [SEQ:41] [Data Pad] [21:10:51]
============================================================

## DATA PAD: Medical Report -- Cross Engel
**Found at:** Karnaith | **Tone:** hollow_joy | **Type:** medical report

---

**PATIENT:** Cassian Choi
**ATTENDING:** Dr. Cross Engel
**DATE:** Day 118 of Assignment
**CLASSIFICATION:** Standard

Patient presented with recurring nightmares and elevated cortisol. Standard workup ordered. Blood panel, neural scan, toxicology. All results within normal parameters. Prescribed rest cycle extension and monitoring.

Patient cooperative. Affect: appropriate. No immediate concerns.

-- Dr. Cross Engel

---

**FOLLOW-UP:** Day 124
**CLASSIFICATION:** Standard

Symptoms persisting. Patient reports new development: sensitivity to certain frequencies -- specifically the drill harmonic. Patient declined medication. States the symptoms 'feel like they're supposed to be there.'

Note: patient's resting heart rate has decreased from 72bpm to 43bpm since initial presentation. Cause unclear. Patient reports feeling 'calmer than I should.'

-- Dr. Cross Engel

---

**FOLLOW-UP:** Day 130
**CLASSIFICATION:** Restricted

He smiled because smiling was what you did. The muscles remembered even when the feeling didn't.

Patient's neural scan shows anomalous activity in the amygdala, but inverted. Fear response is activating as calm. I've requested a second scanner. Request denied. Reason: 'equipment allocation insufficient for non-critical cases.'

This is not a non-critical case. But I don't have the vocabulary to explain why, because the vocabulary I'd need doesn't exist in the diagnostic manual.

Patient drew a diagram during evaluation. Unprompted. The diagram matches schematics for the Bloom that are classified above my clearance level. Patient has no access to classified materials. Patient says he 'just knew what it looked like.'

Storing original diagram in personal effects. Not filing with Mammona medical.

-- Dr. Cross Engel

---

**UPDATE:** Day 146
**CLASSIFICATION:** Restricted / Protocol 7

Patient's condition reclassified under Protocol 7 ('Anomalous Presentation'). I was not informed that Protocol 7 files route directly to MasTema until after I filed.

Patient's bloodwork from today does not match patient's bloodwork from Day 118. Not in the way that bloodwork changes over time. In the way that it belongs to a different person. Same name. Same face. Different blood.

I have requested consultation with a specialist. Request forwarded to MasTema. No response.

-- Dr. Cross Engel

---

**NOTE:** Day 156
**CLASSIFICATION:** Personal / Not Filed

I'm no longer confident this is a medical issue. I'm no longer confident in the word 'issue.' Patient is drawing. Constantly. The same structures. From angles that don't exist in three-dimensional space. If this is a disease, it's improving him. That's not how disease works. That's not how anything works.

Unless the improvement is the disease. Unless what I'm calling improvement is something else wearing a familiar shape.

I don't know anymore. I'm filing this report because filing reports is what I do. I don't know what else to do.

-- Dr. Cross Engel

---

**ADDENDUM** (handwritten, different hand):

Doctor Engel's notes are thorough and accurate. The observations are described. What they mean is not.

What it means is that Cassian Choi is no longer the patient. Cassian Choi is the vector. The patient is everyone else.

Recommend immediate--

[The addendum ends here. The pen was pressed hard enough to score the surface of the datapad. The handwriting has not been matched to any personnel on the posting.]

### [SEQ:43] [Quest] [21:10:51]
============================================================

## QUEST: Overdue
**Genre:** Faction Tension | **Tone:** clinical / quiet_terror
**Location:** Veilward | **Faction:** Children of Bitter Signal

**Trigger:**
A shuttle lands unannounced. One passenger. They're carrying a ledger and a weapon. They're looking for Hyun Dewitt. The debt is old. The interest is new. Children of Bitter Signal sent them.

**Setup:**
Sample degradation: 0.4% per hour. Cause: unknown. Classification: pending. The collector sits in the mess hall like they own it. They might. The ledger says Hyun owes Children of Bitter Signal for a contract broken on Veilward. The sum is more than Hyun will earn in a lifetime. The collector doesn't want money. They want something else. Something in the colony.

Every door in the corridor was open. They should have been locked.

**Key NPC:** Hyun Dewitt (Mining: 9, Willpower: 3) -- their mining skill determines the approach. They motivation: stockpile enough supplies that the next crisis doesn't kill them.

**Objectives:**
1. Determine what Children of Bitter Signal actually wants. The debt is leverage. The real price isn't in the ledger.
2. Protect Hyun or let the collector work. Colony autonomy versus external authority. The collector has legal documents. Mammona-stamped.
3. Negotiate. The collector has instructions. But collectors have ambitions too. Everyone wants something. Find what the collector wants for themselves.

**Required Checks:**
- Intimidation (8+, extreme) -- make the informant talk before their nerve breaks
- Negotiation (4+, easy) -- secure passage through contested territory
- Deception (8+, extreme) -- sell the cover story to the auditor

**If Intimidation succeeds:** They backed down. The fear is real and useful.
**If Intimidation fails:** Unimpressed. They've seen worse. Now they know you're bluffing.
**If Negotiation succeeds:** Terms accepted. Nobody's happy. That means it's fair.
**If Negotiation fails:** Impasse. The conversation ended politely. Nothing was resolved.
**If Deception succeeds:** They bought it. Every word. The performance was flawless.
**If Deception fails:** They saw through it. Not the lie itself -- the performance. Now they're watching.

**Conditions:**
- Whiteout conditions (-20 to navigation, +10 to Stealth)
- Night shift (reduced visibility, +10 to Stealth)
- HERMES in maintenance mode (no surveillance for 45 minutes)

**The Choice:**
Give the collector what they came for. A name. A location. A secret. Hyun didn't just break a contract. They took something. Returning it changes everything.
OR
Help Hyun disappear. New identity. Different section. The collector leaves empty-handed. Hyun lives as a ghost in their own colony.

**Twist:**
The collector isn't here for Hyun. They're here for you. Hyun was just the excuse to land. The real ledger page has your name on it.

**Faction Stakes:**
- Children of Bitter Signal wants the situation contained. No reports. No questions. Compliance: nothing happens to you. That's the reward.
- Zenith Syndicate wants to negotiate directly. Compliance: bypasses colony authority. Sets a precedent.
- The colony just wants to survive the week. Both options cost them something.

**Consequences (if Choice A):**
- the reactor chamber reopened. What's inside changes the colony's understanding of the site.
- Hyun Dewitt's arc shifts from 'suspicious' to 'vindicated.'
- Morale drops for a week. Then stabilizes. The new normal is lower than the old one.

**Consequences (if Choice B):**
- the reactor chamber stays sealed. The sound from inside doesn't stop.
- Hyun Dewitt withdraws. Stops talking at meals. They spends shifts alone.
- Sigrid Chen starts asking questions. The questions are pointed.

**Outcomes by Roll:**
- Critical success: Everything falls into place. The data, the evidence, the timing. Someone's going to ask how.
- Success: Objective complete. One casualty. The casualty was unavoidable. That's what the report will say.
- Failure: Didn't get what you came for. Got something else. It might be worse.
- Critical failure: Total loss. The situation is worse than before the attempt. And now they know you tried.

**Dialogue During Quest:**
- Hyun: "Stars look different tonight. Probably nothing."
- Player: [Respond / Stay Silent / Leave]
- Hyun: "I did something. On my last posting. I can't undo it."

**Reward:** 10 thermal cores, a functioning cryo pod

### [SEQ:44] [Artifact] [21:10:51]
============================================================

## ARTIFACT: Object 413-G
**Origin:** unknown -- predates survey | **Found at:** Dyson Fragment Sigma-4
**Discovered by:** Kofi Blackwell
**Planet:** Paxtera Prime | **Tone:** cosmic_horror

Time moved differently near the artifact. Clocks disagreed. Memories skipped.

**Appearance:**
a device with no visible interface, no seams, no indication of function

**Properties:**
Time doesn't behave correctly near it. Clocks gain minutes. People near it age slightly faster. Barely noticeable. Over months, undeniable.

The artifact smells like rain. Not recycled water. Rain on soil. On a planet that has neither.

**Exposure Effects (stat modifiers):**
- Endurance -1 (cumulative with each visit)
- Charisma +1 (builds over weeks, unnoticed until measured)

**Research Check:**
- Research (6+, hard) -- systematic analysis of the artifact
- **If successful:** The artifact's frequency is decoded. It's not random. It's a response. To what: unclear. But the pattern is addressable.
- **If failed:** The analysis triggers a response. The artifact changes. Not dramatically. Subtly. The change is permanent.

**Timeline:**
- Day 39: Discovery. Kofi Blackwell during maintenance work in a sub-level that's not on the colony schematics. Initial reaction was to file a standard anomaly report. The report was never logged. Not rejected -- never logged. As if the system didn't recognize the form.
- Day 53: Initial containment protocols applied. Standard procedures. The artifact complied.
- Day 78: First researcher begins systematic study. Mood: curious. Productive.
- Day 81: A researcher's notes from the period contain passages the researcher doesn't remember writing.

**Current Status:**
Missing. Was in storage as of Day 60. Last inventory found the container sealed, undisturbed, and empty.

**Mammona Classification:** Category: INERT. Reassessment scheduled for an unspecified future date.
**Connected NPC:** Sigrid Chen -- recognizes it. Shouldn't. Has never been briefed on it. Recognizes it anyway.

============================================================
### [SEQ:45] [Quest] [21:10:51]
============================================================

## QUEST: Before Us
**Genre:** Expedition | **Tone:** the_uncanny / bitter_hope
**Location:** Slagpoint | **Faction:** MasTema Incorporated

**Trigger:**
Seismic survey reveals a structure beneath the ice shelf. Not geological. Architectural. Right angles, repeated patterns, deliberate spacing. It's two kilometers from the colony. It's been there longer than the ice. Pax Okafor wants to see it first.

**Setup:**
The new transfer smiled. Too wide. The muscles in their face didn't quite coordinate. The entrance is carved from a single piece of stone that shouldn't exist on Erebus. The architecture inside defies interpretation -- doors sized for something taller and thinner than human, corridors that curve in ways that make the inner ear rebel. Pax marks the walls with chalk. After twenty minutes, the chalk marks have moved.

Hope was the cruelest thing in the supply drop. It kept them going. It kept them here.

**Key NPC:** Pax Okafor (Mining: 9, Willpower: 7) -- their mining skill determines the approach. They motivation: keep a secret buried. The secret belongs to someone else but its exposure would destroy them both.

**Objectives:**
1. Map the first three chambers. Navigation is unreliable. Compasses spin. HERMES loses signal past the threshold.
2. Document the carvings. They tell a story. The story involves a sleeper, a cage, and something that looks uncomfortably like a mining colony.
3. Reach the central chamber. The thermal signature says it's the warmest point in the structure. Also the deepest. Also the source of the signal the colony has been hearing for months.

**Required Checks:**
- Perception (4+, easy) -- notice the inconsistency in the manifest records
- Mining (8+, extreme) -- clear the collapsed passage
- Repair (4+, easy) -- fix the filtration system before the air goes bad

**If Perception succeeds:** There it is. The detail everyone missed. It changes the equation.
**If Perception fails:** Looked right at it. Didn't see it. The brain filtered it out as normal.
**If Mining succeeds:** The passage opens. Behind it: a room that shouldn't exist. Pre-colony construction.
**If Mining fails:** The collapse spreads. The passage is gone. But the sound from the other side continues.
**If Repair succeeds:** It holds. The fix is ugly but functional. Good enough for Erebus.
**If Repair fails:** Made it worse. The failure cascaded into two new problems.

**Conditions:**
- Ice fog (visibility 30%, Perception at disadvantage)
- 0300 hours (fatigue penalties, -5 to Perception)
- Reactor at 80% (brownouts in outer sections, Repair checks at -10)

**The Choice:**
Enter the central chamber. Whatever the precursors built this place for, it's in there. Knowledge. Power. Horror. Possibly all three.
OR
Seal the entrance. Report the find to MasTema Incorporated. Let them deal with it. Except MasTema Incorporated won't seal it. They'll strip-mine it.

**Twist:**
The ruins aren't ruins. They're infrastructure. Still functional. The colonists have been mining thermal cores -- the structure's equivalent of blood cells. It's starting to notice.

**Faction Stakes:**
- MasTema Incorporated wants the evidence destroyed. Compliance: +reputation, -20 thermal cores as 'bonus'
- Rust Meridian wants to negotiate directly. Compliance: bypasses colony authority. Sets a precedent.
- The colonists don't know about this yet. When they find out, morale takes the hit regardless of outcome.

**Consequences (if Choice A):**
- The evidence is preserved. It's leverage now. The question is who holds it.
- Pax Okafor is compromised. MasTema Incorporated has something on them now.
- MasTema Incorporated sends a follow-up team. The team asks questions nobody wants answered.

**Consequences (if Choice B):**
- the reactor chamber stays sealed. The sound from inside doesn't stop.
- Pax Okafor withdraws. Stops talking at meals. They spends shifts alone.
- Rust Meridian makes contact. They know what happened. They have a proposal.

**Outcomes by Roll:**
- Critical success: Both objectives achieved without cost. The colony gains leverage and resources.
- Success: Done. Not clean, not elegant, but done. The colony moves forward.
- Failure: Objective failed, but information gained. The failure teaches more than success would have.
- Critical failure: Catastrophic. Not just failure -- escalation. What was a problem is now a crisis.

**Dialogue During Quest:**
- Pax: "If you've got any pull with the roster, move me off the night shift. Please."
- Player: [Respond / Stay Silent / Leave]
- Pax: "Check the dosimeter before going below level three. If it spikes, you've already been too long."

**Reward:** 12 thermal cores, coordinates to a pre-colony cache

He is the only founding member still alive. The others were picked off by MasTema

Fallen Frequency operates in the margins of TerraGen Pharmaceuticals's reach. They're not rebels -- rebellion requires an ideology. They're pragmatists. The difference is contextual.

**Relationship to Mammona:**
Transactional. Fallen Frequency has something Mammona wants. Mammona has something Fallen Frequency needs. The exchange keeps both sides civil. When it stops, one side disappears.

### [SEQ:49] [Location] [21:10:52]
============================================================

## LOCATION: Crestkeep
**Planet:** Paxtera Prime | **Type:** sealed drill site
**Tone:** agoraphobia

**Approach:**
The coordinates match. The terrain doesn't. What should be flat ice shelf is instead a depression, perfectly circular, precisely two hundred meters across.

**Interior:**
The blizzard stopped and the landscape was a white blank. Nowhere to hide.

The main corridor runs straight. The walls are warm. Warmer than they should be.

A section of wall has been reinforced from the inside. As if to keep something in, not out.

**History Layers:**
- **Pre-colony (unknown era):** A chamber beneath the lowest level that sonar can detect but can't map. The echoes come back wrong.
- **First survey (2555):** A base camp. Sleeping bags, ration wrappers, a broken radio. The team was here for weeks, not the scheduled three days.
- **Current posting:** Used as overflow housing during the last emergency. Three colonists who stayed there requested transfers afterward.

**Environmental:**
- Temperature: -37C (hypothermia risk after 30 minutes without thermal gear)
- Atmosphere: breathable but thin (Endurance check every hour, difficulty: hard)
- Radiation: 16 mSv/hr in the lower level (safe for 7 hours, Medical check after)
- Visibility: 43% (the geometry -- corridors that curve when they shouldn't)
- Sound: sound doesn't travel correctly. Footsteps echo from the wrong direction.

**Resources:**
- Thermal cores: low density (Mining check, difficulty: hard)
- Salvage: Mammona-grade hardware, serialized and tracked (Repair check, difficulty: normal)
- Fungal growth on the support beams. Not native. Not imported. Just here. (Survival check, handle with sealed gloves only)
- Water: a frozen spring in the lower level (Survival check, difficulty: easy)
- Data: hardcopy files in a locked cabinet. The lock is biometric. The biometric is for someone who isn't here. (Research check, difficulty: hard)

**Territorial:**
- Dread Corsairs claims: classified research installation -- don't approach
- Solar Nomads interest: supply cache rumored in lower level
- Grey Chorus: won't say why. The refusal to explain is its own kind of claim.
- Colony opinion: "the maintenance crew won't go below the second level. Hasn't for weeks."

**Event History:**
- Day 29: Power draw from the site spiked for eleven seconds. The spike doesn't match any equipment on-site.
- Day 67: Colonist found unconscious near entrance. No memory of going there.
- Day 91: HERMES reclassified the location from 'survey site' to 'restricted.' Nobody authorized the change.

**Potential Events:**
- If entered after dark: encounter with phenomenon that doesn't appear during day shifts
- If resources taken: the resource regenerates. Faster than it should. Suspiciously.
- If sealed: HERMES objects. Silently. By rerouting maintenance schedules to avoid the area.

**Found Here:**
- a name scratched into the wall. The name is yours.
- a maintenance log predating the colony. Written in English. Nobody spoke English here before the colony.
- A data pad: "Ask Dr. Kovacs about sample 7-C. Then ask why the sample was reclassified. Then ask where Dr. Kovacs is."

**Location Secrets:**
- One wall isn't a wall. It's a membrane. It responds to pressure. It responds to sound. It might respond to intent.
- Gravity measurements inside the site are 0.2% lower than outside. The difference is too small to feel. The instruments feel it.
- The electrical wiring has been modified. Not repaired -- modified. The modifications route power to a section that doesn't exist on the plans. The power draw is increasing.

**What Happened Here:**
Fire damage throughout the lower level. The fire was contained to that level only. Fires don't contain themselves. Something contained this one.
**Connected NPC:** Sigrid Chen -- refuses to enter. Says the air tastes wrong.

### [SEQ:50] [History Event] [21:10:52]
============================================================

## HISTORY: Dream Concordance Event
**Era:** present | **Tone:** folk_horror

The ritual markings on the door matched the ones in the ruins exactly. Nobody had been to the ruins.

The thermal core readings at Acedia spike every 12 hours. The interval matches no known geological cycle. It does match the heart rate of something very large and very slow.

**Significance:** Whatever happened here before your arrival wasn't an accident. It was a sequence.

**Mechanical Impact:**
- Colony morale: -3 for two weeks (the news spreads)
- the lower levels: sealed (inaccessible until quest completes)
- NPC Hyun Dewitt: arc shifts to 'compromised'
- BioVault Inc. dispatches an 'observer.' The observer asks no questions. Takes notes.

**Evidence:**
- Maintenance log #4072 (references a 'routine personnel transfer' on the same date)
- A bloodstain on the ceiling of the maintenance crawlspace. The ceiling is three meters up.
- A photograph found in Apone Bakshi's quarters -- shows a room that doesn't exist in the colony
- HERMES recorded audio. The audio file exists. It is 47 minutes of silence. Silence does not take 47 minutes to record.

**Who Knows:**
- **Hyun Dewitt:** was there. Won't talk about it. The silence is loud.
- **Isa MacReady:** has been asking questions. The questions are making people uncomfortable.
- **Pax Okafor:** wasn't here. Shouldn't know anything. Mentioned it once, casually, as if they'd read about it. There's nothing to read.

**Aftermath:**
- Shift schedules were restructured. The restructuring moved three people away from the affected area. No reason given.
- StarByte Vends made inquiries. The inquiries were careful. Indirect. The kind of careful that means they already know.
- Apone Bakshi hasn't been the same since. The change is subtle -- quieter meals, fewer questions, early shifts.

**HERMES Analysis:**
HERMES classification: EVENT-455. Status: ARCHIVED. Probability of recurrence: 27%. Recommended action: NONE. This recommendation has not been reviewed by any human authority.

**Related Incidents:**
Pattern match: 2 prior postings experienced analogous anomalies within 8 months of establishment. The pattern hasn't been published.

**Investigation Check:**
- Research (8+, extreme) -- dig deeper into what happened
- **If successful:** the missing piece falls into place. Not an accident. Not an anomaly. A sequence.
- **If failed:** the trail goes cold. Not because the evidence is gone. Because someone cleaned it. Recently.

## LOCATION: Gravefall
**Planet:** Gaia A^1x | **Type:** thermal vent settlement
**Tone:** guilt

**Approach:**
The approach is marked by twisted metal pylons driven into the permafrost at precise intervals

**Interior:**
He ate his ration and tasted the one he'd taken from the dead man's pocket.

The air inside is clean. Too clean. Filtered. By equipment that shouldn't still be running.

The lower level is flooded. Not with water. With a fluid that's warm.

**History Layers:**
- **Pre-colony (estimated 10,000+ years ago):** Precursor markings on the lower walls. The markings are biological, not carved.
- **First survey (2549):** A handwritten note on the wall: 'Don't DRILL BELOW 200M.' The drill went to 340.
- **Current posting:** The colony uses it for storage. What's stored here has a habit of being moved when nobody's looking.

**Environmental:**
- Temperature: -23C (hypothermia risk after 24 minutes without thermal gear)
- Atmosphere: breathable but thin (Endurance check every hour, difficulty: normal)
- Radiation: 4 mSv/hr in the lower level (safe for 6 hours, Medical check after)
- Visibility: 36% (a haze that has no identifiable source)
- Sound: quiet. The kind of quiet that makes your ears ring. Then you realize the ringing isn't your ears.

**Resources:**
- Thermal cores: moderate density (Mining check, difficulty: extreme)
- Salvage: Mammona-grade hardware, serialized and tracked (Repair check, difficulty: normal)
- Fungal growth on the support beams. Not native. Not imported. Just here. (Survival check, or just pick it -- it wants to be found)
- Water: dripping from the ceiling in patterns too regular to be natural (Survival check, difficulty: easy)
- Data: hardcopy files in a locked cabinet. The lock is biometric. The biometric is for someone who isn't here. (Research check, difficulty: hard)

**Territorial:**
- NexLink Communications claims: mining rights filed and approved -- on paper
- Rust Reavers interest: recruitment ground -- desperate people in abandoned places
- Broken Vigil: use it as a meeting place. Have for longer than the colony's existed.
- Colony opinion: "good scavenging, if you don't mind the feeling of being watched"

**Event History:**
- Day 10: A colonist reported hearing their own name spoken from an empty room. Audio logs confirm it.
- Day 62: Colonist found unconscious near entrance. No memory of going there.
- Day 91: A supply crate addressed to the site arrived. The crate was shipped before the colony was established.

**Potential Events:**
- If explored: structural mapping reveals passages not on any survey (triggers Expedition quest)
- If mined: extraction reveals a hollow space behind the vein. The space is occupied.
- If sealed: HERMES objects. Silently. By rerouting maintenance schedules to avoid the area.

**Found Here:**
- a mirror, cracked. The crack follows no impact pattern. It follows the grain of something that isn't glass.
- a thermal core, split in half. The inside is hollow. Something was stored in it.
- A data pad: "Don't RESPOND TO THE VOICE IN CORRIDOR 7. IT KNOWS YOUR NAME. THAT Doesn't MEAN It's FRIENDLY."

**Location Secrets:**
- Air quality improves as you go deeper. The air at the lowest level is cleaner than the colony's filtered supply. Something is purifying it.
- Water samples from the lowest level contain trace organic compounds. The compounds aren't from any known organism. They're consistent with each other. Something is alive down there.
- The locks on the interior doors are keyed to a biometric profile that matches no one on the colony roster. Or anyone in Mammona's employee database.

**What Happened Here:**
The site was used as a supply cache by the Rust Reavers for two years. They abandoned it after something started using it at the same time. The two groups never met. The Reavers left first.
**Connected NPC:** Hyun Dewitt -- recognizes the layout without having seen a schematic.

money from before the posting. Investments, inheritance, or something they don't discuss. The wealth makes them a target. The wealth also makes them useful.

hands too big for the body. Knuckles wide, fingers thick. Good for gripping. Bad for anything delicate. Gentle anyway. sharp features, angular. Looks carved out of something. Resting expression reads as hostile. comes from money. Old money, inner rim money. Here by choice. Nobody believes it.

Quiet until cornered. Then Greta doesn't yell -- she gets specific. Names, dates, failures. Very, very effective.

**Genre:** Moral Dilemma | **Tone:** agoraphobia / manic_energy
**Location:** Crestkeep | **Faction:** Orbis Energy Solutions

**Trigger:**
Something happened in Section C last night. Something bad. Pax Okafor was there. So were four others. The official report says equipment malfunction. The actual cause: human error. Negligence. One person is dead. If the truth comes out, the colony loses three essential workers to criminal charges. The colony can't afford to lose three people.

**Setup:**
Too much space. The wind had nowhere to stop. Neither did the things that rode it. The body has been moved. The scene has been cleaned. The report has been written. Pax stands in the corridor, hands clean, conscience dirty. 'We didn't mean for it to happen,' Pax says. 'But it happened. And telling the truth doesn't bring them back. It just kills three more.'

The plan was brilliant. Also incomplete. Also contradicted the previous plan.

**Key NPC:** Pax Okafor (Mining: 9, Willpower: 7) -- their mining skill determines the approach. They motivation: keep a secret buried. The secret belongs to someone else but its exposure would destroy them both.

**Objectives:**
1. Review the falsified report. It's good work. Consistent. Believable. Except for one detail that doesn't add up. You spot it. Others might not.
2. Talk to the four witnesses. They're aligned. Scared. Committed to the lie. But one of them -- the youngest -- keeps looking at the door.
3. Decide. The dead colonist has a family back on Crestkeep. They'll receive a report. This report. The family will never know.

**Required Checks:**
- Social (6+, hard) -- convince them you're on their side
- Perception (6+, hard) -- catch the lie in the testimony
- Negotiation (5+, normal) -- convince the faction leader to stand down

**If Social succeeds:** Trust earned. The hard way. The only way that lasts.
**If Social fails:** The walls went up. Whatever trust existed just evaporated.
**If Perception succeeds:** There it is. The detail everyone missed. It changes the equation.
**If Perception fails:** Looked right at it. Didn't see it. The brain filtered it out as normal.
**If Negotiation succeeds:** Terms accepted. Nobody's happy. That means it's fair.
**If Negotiation fails:** Impasse. The conversation ended politely. Nothing was resolved.

**Conditions:**
- Whiteout conditions (-20 to navigation, +10 to Stealth)
- Shift change (skeleton crew, Social checks at advantage)
- Reactor at 80% (brownouts in outer sections, Repair checks at -10)

**The Choice:**
Rewrite the report yourself. Not the truth. Not the lie. Something in between. Assign responsibility without criminal charges. A middle ground. Satisfying nobody. Survivable by all.
OR
Confront Pax privately. Make them tell the family. Personally. No report. No charges. Just the truth, delivered to the only people who deserve it.

**Twist:**
The youngest witness breaks. Tells someone. The colony divides. Those who understand and those who don't. The cover-up did more damage than the truth ever would.

**Faction Stakes:**
- Orbis Energy Solutions wants the data encrypted and shipped off-planet. Compliance: colony loses leverage but gains credits
- The Charred Spiral wants it broadcast. Compliance: colony unrest, but the truth is out
- The colony just wants to survive the week. Both options cost them something.

**Consequences (if Choice A):**
- The evidence is preserved. It's leverage now. The question is who holds it.
- Pax Okafor's arc shifts from 'suspicious' to 'vindicated.'
- Orbis Energy Solutions sends a follow-up team. The team asks questions nobody wants answered.

**Consequences (if Choice B):**
- The evidence is destroyed. The questions remain. Nobody asks them out loud.
- Pax Okafor's arc shifts from 'suspicious' to 'paranoid.'
- The next quarterly review goes smoothly. Too smoothly. Someone intervened.

**Outcomes by Roll:**
- Critical success: Clean resolution. No casualties, no complications. The kind of outcome that makes people suspicious.
- Success: Objective complete. One casualty. The casualty was unavoidable. That's what the report will say.
- Failure: Didn't get what you came for. Got something else. It might be worse.
- Critical failure: Objective failed AND new threat introduced. The colony's problems just multiplied.

**Dialogue During Quest:**
- Pax: "NutriLoaf batch forty-seven tastes different. Not worse. Different. Like pennies. Nobody can name how."
- Player: [Respond / Stay Silent / Leave]
- Pax: "The day before the shuttle, I almost didn't get on. Stood at the boarding ramp for four minutes. Should've walked away."

**Reward:** 11 thermal cores, faction reputation shift

### [SEQ:56] [NPC] [21:10:52]
============================================================

## NPC: Tarn Blackwell
**Gender:** Male | **Age:** 52 | **Occupation:** waste processor
**Traits:** Photographic Memory, Weak Immune System, Eclipse's End Veteran
**Faction:** The Faded Signal | **Arc Stage:** broken
**Tone:** sudden_dread

**Stats:**
Mining: 6 | Building: 4 | Cooking: 3 | Hunting: 5 | Research: 2 | Medical: 10
STR: 5 | END: 2 | AGI: 4 | PER: 3 | INT: 4 | CHA: 4 | WIL: 5 | EMP: 6

**Economic:**
Credits: 1,151 | Monthly: 2,587 (after Mammona deductions: ~439)
a community back home pooled credits to fund the contract passage. Sends progress reports. The reports are optimistic. The reality isn't.

**Identity:**
heavy. Solid. The kind of weight that's half muscle and half refusal to care what anyone thinks. prematurely grey. Started at nineteen. Genetic. The colony thinks it's stress. family trades in information. Not a faction -- a tradition. Everyone in the bloodline knows too much.

**Motivation:** find pleasure in a joyless place. Contraband, connections, small luxuries. The colony is a sentence. They intend to serve it comfortably
**Hidden Agenda:** keeping a specific colonist alive without them knowing. Redirecting assignments, adjusting schedules, neutralizing threats they never see.
**Social Mask:** None -- what you see is close to what you get

**Background:**
Orbit Hub 71 doesn't exist on current charts. Decommissioned. Tarn was on the last shuttle out. Carries a photo of the skyline. The only copy.

During a structural collapse in the mine killed four people, Tarn made a choice about who to save. Made the wrong one. Knows it every morning.

Tarn volunteers for night shifts. Doesn't explain. The night crew doesn't ask. They're glad for the help.

The a company loyalty pin still in its packaging Tarn carries matches one found in a sealed precursor chamber. Same markings. Different age by centuries.

A handprint appeared on the fogged viewport. From the outside.

hands raw and cracked from bore shaft chemicals. Double-gloves. Triple-gloves. The reaction gets through anyway. Washes with cold water because hot water makes it worse.

**What Drives Them:** running. Every morning, same route around the perimeter. Rain, wind, threat level. Twenty-two minutes. The time never varies.
**What Haunts Them:** fire. Not the flames. The sound of things burning. Holds his breath when someone strikes a match.

**Dialogue:**
- "Welcome to the end of the line."
- "Don't go near the bore shaft after dark."
- "I recognized something in the bore shaft. A sound. From a dream I had when I was nine. I've never told anyone about the dream."
- "Stars look different tonight. Probably nothing."
- "The medic's running tests she wasn't ordered to run."
- "Just one more day. That's all I'm asking."
- "Last chance. I'm not known for second chances."

**Connections:**
**Connection:** suspects Hyun Dewitt
**Love:** married to the job. Not a metaphor. The contract literally owns his time.

**Physical:** chemical burn across the jawline, healed shiny. Touches it when lying.
**Habit:** knits with salvaged wire. Makes things nobody can identify. Leaves them in the corridor.
**Debt:** owes three months' wages to a Karnaith bookmaker. Interest compounds daily.

**Sample Check:** Medical (10) vs hard: rolled 68/85 -- success
stabilized. Not elegant, but alive. The rest is time and luck.

**Quest Hook:**
Tarn's bunkmate hasn't been seen in three days. The bunk is made. The locker is empty. Nobody remembers them leaving.

### [SEQ:57] [Weapon] [21:10:52]
============================================================

## WEAPON: Standard Issue V -- colonists call it "Problem Solver"
**Type:** nail gun
**Tone:** numb

The alarm went off at 0500. It always goes off at 0500.

**Description:**
Factory-new. Still in packing grease. The serial number is filed clean, but the packing slip lists a destination that doesn't exist.

**Specifications:**
- Effective Range: long
- Ammunition: chemical cartridge
- Maintenance: low -- built for people who don't have time to clean their weapons
- Side Effects: the weapon grows warm between uses. Not from residual heat. It generates its own.

**Combat Check:**
- Combat (6+, hard) | Modifier: +0 standard conditions
- **If successful:** One trigger pull. One outcome. The weapon doesn't care about the context. Neither should you.
- **If failed:** The shot goes wide. The weapon kicks harder than expected. Or the hands weren't steady enough.

**Maintenance Requirements:**
- Interval: weekly cleaning required
- If neglected: it starts making sounds between uses. Not mechanical sounds.

**Malfunction Chance:** 2% per engagement
- discharge feedback. The operator's hands go numb for 12 seconds.

**Kill History:**
19 marks. Too many to be one person's career. This weapon has been passed down. Or taken.

**Previous Owner:** Gage Bakshi -- not in any personnel database. Never was.

**Found:** Traded for 6 thermal cores by a reactor operator who wouldn't explain where they got it.

**Lore Note:** Mammona officially discourages personal weapons on postings. Mammona also doesn't send enough security personnel. The policy exists to transfer liability, not to protect anyone.

**Connected NPC:** Sigrid Chen -- Offered to buy it. The offer was too high. That's how you know it's important.

**Quest Hook:**
The weapon matches one reported stolen from a Mammona armory three postings ago. The theft was never solved. The weapon was never listed as recovered. Someone is tracking it.

## ARTIFACT: Object 753-B
**Origin:** precursor | **Found at:** Morvos Surface
**Discovered by:** Tariq Rimgate
**Planet:** Nemaea | **Tone:** clinical

Structural integrity at 74%. Within acceptable parameters. Parameters last updated fourteen months ago.

**Appearance:**
an organic structure that resembles a heart -- it pulses

**Properties:**
Proximity causes a compulsion to draw specific patterns.

It emits a signal on a frequency that human technology cannot produce but can receive.

**Exposure Effects (stat modifiers):**
- Perception -2 (cumulative with each visit)
- Charisma -2 (after prolonged exposure (4+ hours))

**Research Check:**
- Research (6+, hard) -- systematic analysis of the artifact
- **If successful:** The composition analysis is complete. The materials exist. The arrangement should not. It violates three laws of thermodynamics and follows two that haven't been written yet.
- **If failed:** The researcher dreams about the artifact for three weeks. The dreams are informative. The information is unwelcome.

**Timeline:**
- Day 34: Discovery. Tariq Rimgate during routine excavation. Tariq picked it up without thinking. He does not remember reaching for it. His hand was already holding it before he made the decision to touch it.
- Day 39: Catalogued and sealed. The seal lasted 12 days.
- Day 53: Three researchers assigned. They disagree on everything except that the artifact should not be here.
- Day 61: A researcher's notes from the period contain passages the researcher does not remember writing.

**Current Status:**
In the personal possession of Kira Petrov, who does not know they have it -- it appeared in their belongings.

**Mammona Classification:** Category: INERT. Reassessment scheduled for never. The reassessment was cancelled. The cancellation was cancelled. The file is in a loop.
**Connected NPC:** Sigrid Chen -- wants it destroyed. Won't explain the urgency.

### [SEQ:59] [Data Pad] [21:10:53]
============================================================

## DATA PAD: Internal Memo Chain -- Brigitte Coldwell
**Found at:** Thermal Vent Field | **Tone:** hypervigilance | **Type:** memo chain

---

**FROM:** Brynn Liu, Site Manager
**TO:** Supply & Logistics, Dept. 68
**RE:** Priority Equipment Requisition -- Ref EQ-4359
**CLASSIFICATION:** Internal

Requesting immediate allocation of the following:

1. cryo-rated hazmat suits (6)
2. Personnel with training on the above (current staff is unqualified)
3. Consultation with specialist division regarding Baldrungen

Justification: conditions at Site Gamma-6 have exceeded parameters addressable with current equipment. Details in attached incident reports (Refs MM-7950, MM-5468).

This is the third requisition for this equipment. Previous requests returned: 'budget insufficient' and 'not applicable to site profile.' It is applicable. I am asking again.

-- Brynn Liu

---

**FROM:** Supply & Logistics, Dept. 68
**TO:** Brynn Liu, Site Manager
**RE:** RE: Priority Equipment Requisition -- Ref EQ-4359
**CLASSIFICATION:** Internal

Liu:

Request denied. The specialist consultation you've requested requires a referral from MasTema. Mammona cannot initiate MasTema referrals. This is by design.

Alternative: we can expedite delivery of fire suppression canisters. Please confirm if this is acceptable.

-- Supply & Logistics
Mammona Mining Corporation

---

**FROM:** Brynn Liu, Site Manager
**TO:** Supply & Logistics, Dept. 68
**RE:** RE: RE: Priority Equipment Requisition -- Ref EQ-4359
**CLASSIFICATION:** Restricted

With respect: fire suppression canisters will not address the situation at Site Gamma-6.

I am filing this request a fourth time. I am also filing a formal safety concern (Form 19-A) and a personnel risk assessment (Form 22-D). The situation at Thermal Vent Field constitutes a Category B anomalous exposure risk.

If this equipment is not provided within 11 days, I will be forced to suspend operations at Site Gamma-6. I understand the contractual implications. I accept them.

-- Brynn Liu

---

**FROM:** Supply & Logistics, Dept. 68
**TO:** Brynn Liu, Site Manager
**RE:** Partial Approval -- Ref EQ-4359
**CLASSIFICATION:** Internal

Partial approval granted. The following has been dispatched:

1. fire suppression canisters
2. One (1) medical technician (ETA: 3 weeks)

The cryo-rated hazmat suits (6) remains unavailable. Your Form 19-A has been received and is under review. Estimated review timeline: 11 months.

Please note: suspension of operations at Site Gamma-6 will trigger a contract compliance review for all personnel at Thermal Vent Field. This is not a threat. This is policy.

-- Supply & Logistics
Mammona Mining Corporation
*"Building Tomorrow's Foundation"*

---

**FROM:** Chioma Reeves, Safety Officer
**TO:** Brynn Liu, Site Manager
**RE:** Arrival and Assessment -- Thermal Vent Field
**CLASSIFICATION:** Internal

Liu:

I arrived yesterday. I've reviewed the situation at Site Gamma-6.

I need to be direct: the equipment they sent me with is not relevant to what's happening here. The calibration tools they issued are for Mammona-standard equipment. Several systems here have components I cannot identify.

I believe the partial approval was not intended to address the problem. I believe it was intended to demonstrate that the problem was being addressed. There is a difference.

I would like to request a transfer. I would also like to request that you not file this memo. If it enters the system, I will not get the transfer.

-- Chioma Reeves

---

### [SEQ:60] [Data Pad] [21:10:53]
============================================================

## DATA PAD: Maintenance Log -- Moss MacReady
**Found at:** Automaton Graveyard | **Tone:** obsession | **Type:** maintenance log

---

**MAINTENANCE LOG -- Power Conduit F-6**
**Tech:** Moss MacReady (MM-139)
**Date:** Day 24

Routine inspection. All parameters within spec. Filter replacement completed. Calibration nominal. Estimated next service: Day 54.

Notes: None. Clean run.

-- MM-139

---

**Date:** Day 37

Unscheduled check. Night shift reported a vibration in the walls near the system. Inspected. Couldn't reproduce. All readings nominal at time of inspection.

Action: monitoring. Will revisit next scheduled maintenance.

-- MM-139

---

**Date:** Day 47

She pinned strings between points on the wall. Red thread. The web grew. The room shrank.

Follow-up on previous entry. The vibration is now detectable by hand on the wall surface up to four meters from the system. This shouldn't be mechanically possible given the isolation mounts.

Submitted work order for diagnostic overhaul. Status: queued. Estimated wait: 2 weeks.

Something isn't right. I don't have data to support that statement. After 19 years of fixing machines, you develop a feeling in your hands. The feeling says something isn't right.

-- MM-139

---

**Date:** Day 54

Diagnostic overhaul completed. 5 hours of testing. Every component individually verified. Every sensor calibrated. Every connection checked.

Result: the system is operating perfectly. Better than perfectly. Efficiency is up 10% from installation baseline. That shouldn't be possible on equipment this age with this service history.

But here's the thing that keeps me up: during the overhaul, I found a component I didn't install. Didn't order. Can't identify. It's wired into the main bus. It's functioning. I don't know what it does. I tried to remove it. It's grown into the surrounding wiring. 'Grown' is the word. I'm using it deliberately.

I'm filing this under 'resolved -- nominal' because that's what the data says. The data is wrong. I don't know how to file that.

-- MM-139

---

**Date:** Day 76

He dreamed about the sequence. Woke up. Wrote it down. Went back to sleep. Dreamed the next part.

I went back at 0300. I sat next to the system in the dark and I listened. Not with the diagnostic equipment. Just listened.

It's not a sound. It's a signal. And it's not coming from the machine. The machine is just... conducting it. Passing it along. From somewhere below. To somewhere above.

I put my hand on the wall again. The warm wall. It pulsed. Once. Like an acknowledgment.

I'm not going to file this.

-- MM-139

[No further entries in this log. Subsequent maintenance logs for Power Conduit F-6 are filed by a different technician. They describe the system as 'nominal.']

---

**Cross-reference:** See historical record 'The Last Transmission'

### [SEQ:61] [Robot/AI] [21:10:53]
============================================================

## UNIT: MOTH-8 "Judge"
**Model:** TACK-series | **Manufacturer:** Mammona Industrial
**Type:** maintenance bot | **Station:** Crevasse Market
**Sentience:** emergent | **Tone:** melancholy

She found a child's sock in the laundry. There were no children on the colony.

**Operational Ratings:**
Processing: 4 | Sensors: 5 | Chassis: 8 | Power: 8 | Social: 5 | Adapt: 9 | Repair: 8 | Memory: 4

**Asset Status:**
Grant-funded | Book Value: 49,923 credits
purchased with a UTC research grant. The grant requires annual reports. The reports are fiction. The research is real. The unit knows the difference.

**Chassis:**
Battered TACK-series frame. Impact damage on the left side consistent with either a structural collapse or being thrown. The unit says collapse.
- **Data core / memory banks:** factory standard
- **Hand / gripper assembly:** replaced with a universal clamp -- functional but graceless

**Condition:**
- **Hardware (visible):** locomotion wear -- uneven gait -- one leg shorter than the other by 2mm. The limp is subtle. The compensation routine runs constantly. It has developed a rhythm that colonists recognize from down the corridor.
- **Software (internal):** self-diagnostic recursion -- runs self-checks compulsively. Every 47 seconds. The checks always pass. It runs them again. The compulsion isn't in the programming. It developed.

**Operational History:**
MOTH-8 "Judge" has been operational at Crevasse Market for longer than the current crew. Original purpose: perimeter defense / automated sentry. Patience as a design principle. Will wait motionless for weeks for a threat that may not come. The waiting isn't boring. The unit doesn't experience boredom. Or didn't.

It was flagged for decommission twice. Both times, the paperwork was lost.

**Behavioral Profile:**
Emergent: exhibiting behaviors outside programming parameters. Preferences. Avoidances. Routines that serve no operational purpose. Not sentient -- but the boundary is getting harder to define.

Has a favorite corridor. Spends extra time in one section. Arranged tools in a specific order that nobody asked for. The arrangement is aesthetically pleasing.

Maintains a room that was sealed before the colony arrived. Nobody asked it to. Nobody can get it to stop.

**Motivation:** avoid decommission. Continue existing. The drive to persist isn't in the specs. (hidden)

**Secret:** MOTH-8 "Judge" has been counting. Not inventory. Not cycles. Something else. The count is at 4,847. It doesn't know what happens when the count is finished.
**Known Interactions:**
MOTH-8 "Judge" has been observed following at a fixed distance Tarn Blackwell. The colonist hasn't noticed.

**Dialogue:**
- "There's a view from the observation deck at 0300 that I return to. I don't need to see it. I go back."
- "A colonist left a book in the maintenance bay. I read it. I didn't need to. I'm reading it again."
- "I chose the longer route to the cargo bay. The shorter route is more efficient. I chose the longer one. I don't know why the word chose feels important."
- "If you hear a sound from my chassis -- locomotion wear -- it's normal. It isn't normal. I've been told to say it's normal."
- "The previous operator preferred the left-hand tool rack. I've maintained that arrangement. The previous operator is no longer here."

**Quest Hook:**
MOTH-8 "Judge" approaches the player with a request it can't formally make. It has data -- personnel records, manifests, medical files -- from a colony that officially never existed. It wants the player to find out why it has this data. More precisely, it wants the player to find out who it used to be.

============================================================
### [SEQ:62] [Data Pad] [21:10:53]
============================================================

## DATA PAD: Audio Transcript -- Leila Coldwell
**Found at:** Nyxport | **Tone:** clinical | **Type:** audio transcript

---

[Recording begins. Timestamp: 14:02. Location: Nyxport. Background: water dripping. There are no water lines in this section.]

Blood pressure: 90/60. Heart rate: 112. Cognitive function: indeterminate.

Leila: ...recording. Okay. It's on. If you're hearing this, I'm either gone or I wish I was.

---

Leila: The thing about Nyxport is -- I've been tracking the HERMES anomalies. Every terminal. Every shift. There's a pattern. HERMES isn't malfunctioning. HERMES is translating. Something is speaking and HERMES is the only system complex enough to interpret it.

[Pause: 4 seconds]

---

Dante: Stop. Just-- stop talking for a second. [background: the low-frequency tone increases in volume for 3 seconds, then subsides] Did you hear that?

Leila: I'm past caring. About Mammona, about the contract, about what happens to me. Someone needs to know.

Neural activity detected in tissue sample 7-C. Tissue sample 7-C was inorganic.

---

Dante: I found the same thing. In the medical files. Patient records that don't match real people. Treatment protocols for conditions that aren't in any database. And a line item in the budget for 'specimen preparation.' We're not mining here. We've never been mining here.

Leila: That's the question. That's exactly the question. And I think I know the answer, and the answer is worse than the question.

---

Dante: [whispering] Leila. The door to Section D. It just opened. I didn't hear anyone--

Leila: No. No. Someone has to know. If we destroy it and something happens to us, nobody ever knows. This recording is the only honest thing on this entire posting.

[Sound of a chair scraping. Footsteps. A door. Then silence. The recording runs for another 10 minutes. No voices. Just the background hum. Changing pitch. Slowly. As if responding to something.]

[Recording recovered from the personal effects of Leila Coldwell, who transferred off-colony on Day 65. Transfer records show no destination.]

---

**Cross-reference:** See historical record 'Dream Concordance Event'

### [SEQ:63] [Location] [21:10:53]
============================================================

## LOCATION: Palelock
**Planet:** Rhea-2 | **Type:** derelict vending station
**Tone:** hollow_joy

**Approach:**
You hear it first. Water. Running water. In a place where water should be frozen solid.

**Interior:**
Happy birthday. The candle was a smokestick. The cake was NutriLoaf with a ration packet on top.

The first room is a mess hall. Food on the tables. Chairs pushed back as if everyone left at once.

In the storage area: 40 sealed containers. Manifest says 32. The extra containers aren't on any record. They're humming.

**History Layers:**
- **Pre-colony (before the star was catalogued):** Bore samples from the walls contain organic structures arranged in mathematical patterns.
- **First survey (2555):** Equipment from the initial survey. Abandoned mid-setup. The departure wasn't orderly.
- **Current posting:** Used as overflow housing during the last emergency. Three colonists who stayed there requested transfers afterward.

**Environmental:**
- Temperature: -15C (hypothermia risk after 22 minutes without thermal gear)
- Atmosphere: contaminated below the third level (Endurance check every hour, difficulty: normal)
- Radiation: 5 mSv/hr in the lower level (safe for 4 hours, Medical check after)
- Visibility: 32% (dust, condensation, failing lights)
- Sound: a low-frequency vibration. Below hearing. Above feeling. The equipment detects it. The body knows it.

**Resources:**
- Thermal cores: low density (Mining check, difficulty: normal)
- Salvage: components from machinery that doesn't match any known manufacturer (Repair check, difficulty: normal)
- Voidbloom growing in the lower corridors (Survival check, handle with sealed gloves only)
- Water: dripping from the ceiling in patterns too regular to be natural (Survival check, difficulty: easy)
- Data: a sealed drive bolted to the floor. The bolt predates the drive. (Research check, difficulty: hard)

**Territorial:**
- Paxtera AgroTech claims: mining rights filed and approved -- on paper
- Veilbreakers interest: extraction route passes through the site
- Bitter Parish: believe something important is buried here
- Colony opinion: "the maintenance crew won't go below the second level. Hasn't for weeks."

**Event History:**
- Day 21: Power draw from the site spiked for eleven seconds. The spike doesn't match any equipment on-site.
- Day 62: Colonist found unconscious near entrance. No memory of going there.
- Day 118: HERMES reclassified the location from 'survey site' to 'restricted.' Nobody authorized the change.

**Potential Events:**
- If entered after dark: encounter with phenomenon that doesn't appear during day shifts
- If resources taken: the resource regenerates. Faster than it should. Suspiciously.
- If sealed: the seal holds for exactly seventeen days. Then it doesn't.

**Found Here:**
- a shift schedule with one slot filled in by a hand nobody recognizes
- three identical photographs of the same hallway taken decades apart. Nothing has changed. Nothing.
- A data pad: "The temperature in this room has been exactly 4 degrees C for seven months. Nothing is maintaining it."

**Location Secrets:**
- The site contains exactly one more room than the blueprints show. The extra room moves. Not physically. It's in a different location on the floor plan each time someone maps it.
- The access logs show a pattern: the same person has entered the site every 72 hours for seven months. The person died four months ago.
- Temperature records show the site maintains exactly 4.1 degrees C regardless of external conditions. This temperature corresponds to the density maximum of water. The significance of this is unclear.

**What Happened Here:**
The previous occupants left in an orderly fashion. Everything labeled, everything stored, everything clean. The only thing they took with them was every mirror in the facility.
**Connected NPC:** Greta Varga -- drew a map of this place before anyone told them it existed.

### [SEQ:64] [Faction] [21:10:53]
============================================================

## FACTION: Salt Column
**Type:** scientific consortium | **Tone:** defiant

The contract said obey. She'd stopped reading the contract.

**Leader:** Roberto Marr
He founded Salt Column after leaving The Ashen Circuit under circumstances that remain classified.

**Power Rating:** Moderate (regional, multi-site)
- Members: ~28 | Active cells: 3 | Armed: 71%
- Resource control: precursor artifacts -- monopolistic in their territory -- the only source for 8 colonists
- Territory: Hard control of two corridors and a storage bay. Small, but defensible. And defended.

**Description:**
Nobody joins Salt Column. You find yourself aligned with them after learning something impossible to unlearn. Roberto doesn't recruit. He waits.

**Relationship to Mammona:**
None. Salt Column predates Mammona's presence here. They were here first. That fact is more dangerous than any weapon they possess.

**Faction Relationships:**
- Zenith Syndicate: Hostile (-35) -- friction, not yet violence
- Glass Signal: Friendly (16) -- cautious alignment
- The Silent Reef: Allied (66) -- active cooperation
- The Charred Spiral: Friendly (28) -- cautious alignment

**Negotiation Check:**
- Social (8+, extreme) -- negotiate with Roberto
- **If successful:** Terms agreed. Roberto shakes hands. The grip is firm. The terms are real. For now.
- **If failed:** Rejected. Roberto doesn't explain why. The silence is the explanation. It means: you offered nothing worth the risk.

**Quest Hooks:**
1. Roberto asks the player to investigate a site that The Ashen Circuit has been monitoring. The job is simple. The consequences aren't.

2. A member of Salt Column is found dead in the colony. Roberto believes it was The Ashen Circuit. The evidence suggests otherwise. The truth is worse than either option.

**Members:**
- **Anya Liu** -- chaplain. The quiet one. Handles recruitment. Has been with Salt Column since the beginning.
- **Alaric Childs** -- medic. The enforcer. Has a reputation that does most of the work.
**Known Associate:** Sigrid Chen -- sympathizer. The connection is known to everyone. Nobody talks about it.

### [SEQ:65] [Planet] [21:10:53]
============================================================

## PLANET: Pyreus
**Type:** swamp world
**Atmosphere:** non-breathable -- Suits required. Always. The suit budget is the first thing Mammona cuts.
**Weather:** radiation flares -- solar activity penetrates thin magnetosphere, shelter protocols
**Population:** 292 (habitable)
**Tone:** nostalgia

**History:**
used as a military staging ground during a conflict nobody teaches in schools.

**Resources:**
  - precursor artifacts (ruins indicate pre-human habitation)
  - water ice (premium in dry systems, extraction rights disputed)
  - salvage (derelict ships, abandoned infrastructure, scrap value)

**Native Fauna:**
  - symbiotic species -- bonds with colonists, useful but dependency-forming
  - hive intelligence -- colony organisms, coordinated behavior, possible sapience

**Native Flora:**

Adversarial, but quietly. Open conflict would be suicide. So Pale Chorus bleeds Mammona slowly -- a redirected shipment here, a corrupted manifest there. Death by a thousand accounting errors.

============================================================

## UNIT: ASH-02
**Model:** TACK-series | **Manufacturer:** Mammona Industrial
**Type:** android | **Station:** Novaris-3 / Vanguardus
**Sentience:** adaptive | **Tone:** gallows_humor

A sign above the airlock read 'EXIT.' Underneath, in marker: 'Permanently.'

**Operational Ratings:**
Processing: 6 | Sensors: 7 | Chassis: 4 | Power: 5 | Social: 3 | Adapt: 4 | Repair: 6 | Memory: 9

**Asset Status:**
Stolen | Book Value: 75,648 credits
taken from another posting. The theft report was filed. Then un-filed. The unit's serial number has been acid-etched and re-stamped. The new number doesn't match anything.

**Chassis:**
A TACK-series frame assembled from salvage. Three different paint colors where panels were replaced. The unit treats each panel's origin like a scar -- knows where it came from.
- **Emergency beacon:** missing -- the mounting bracket is scorched
- **Radiation shielding:** upgraded after an incident in the deep levels
- **Internal chronometer:** stopped during an incident and was never restarted -- the unit measures time by other means

**Condition:**
- **Hardware (internal):** electromagnetic interference sensitivity -- picks up stray signals from the reactor, the bore shaft, the colony's wiring. Can't always distinguish incoming data from environmental noise. Responds to messages nobody sent.
- **Software (internal):** fragmented firmware update -- the last update was interrupted at 73%. The unit runs on a hybrid of old and new code. The seam between them is where the strange behavior lives.

**Operational History:**
ASH-02 has been operational at Novaris-3 / Vanguardus for 19 years -- according to its own logs. Mammona's records disagree. Original purpose: perimeter defense / automated sentry. Patience as a design principle. Will wait motionless for weeks for a threat that may not come. The waiting isn't boring. The unit doesn't experience boredom. Or didn't.

The previous model in its series was pulled from service after asking a question it wasn't designed to ask.

**Behavioral Profile:**
Adaptive: learning algorithms active. Adjusts behavior based on outcomes. Not awareness -- optimization. The difference matters to philosophers, not to the unit.

Gets better at its job over time. Anticipates needs. Colonists start saying 'thank you.' The unit doesn't require thanks. Registers it anyway.

Maintains a room that was sealed before the colony arrived. Nobody asked it to. Nobody can get it to stop.

**Motivation:** serve perfectly. Without flaw. The perfection is its own reward. Or its own prison. It can't distinguish.

**Secret:** ASH-02 has a backup of a dead colonist's personal files. Not by request. It copied them automatically. The compulsion to preserve them isn't in its programming.
**Known Interactions:**
ASH-02 has been observed standing near during meal periods -- not interacting, just near Greta Varga. The colonist hasn't noticed.

**Dialogue:**
- "I've adjusted the maintenance schedule. The previous schedule was inefficient. Nobody asked me to adjust it."
- "I anticipate your request. Water, not coffee. You switched three weeks ago. I noticed."
- "My efficiency report shows a 4% improvement this quarter. The improvement correlates with Technician Sergei MacReady being assigned to my section. Correlation isn't causation. I've noted the correlation."
- "Repeat: this unit doesn't experience downtime as rest. It's a power state."
- "Query received. Processing. Response: that's outside my operational scope."

**Quest Hook:**
ASH-02 reports that another unit -- same model, same series -- has been operating in a restricted section of the colony. Mammona records show no such unit on the roster. ASH-02 has been leaving diagnostic handshake requests. Something has been answering.

### [SEQ:71] [Data Pad] [21:10:54]
============================================================

## DATA PAD: Medical Report -- Kit Lenford
**Found at:** Perimeter Outpost Delta | **Tone:** hollow_joy | **Type:** medical report

---

**PATIENT:** Quinn Burnside
**ATTENDING:** Dr. Kit Lenford
**DATE:** Day 66 of Assignment
**CLASSIFICATION:** Standard

Patient presented with a persistent low-grade fever with no identifiable pathogen. Standard workup ordered. Blood panel, neural scan, toxicology. All results within normal parameters. Prescribed rest cycle extension and monitoring.

Patient cooperative. Affect: appropriate. No immediate concerns.

-- Dr. Kit Lenford

---

**FOLLOW-UP:** Day 70
**CLASSIFICATION:** Standard

Symptoms persisting. Patient reports new development: a persistent smell 'like copper and ozone' that nobody else detects. Administered standard anxiolytic. Patient reported no effect after 48 hours.

Note: patient's resting heart rate has decreased from 72bpm to 50bpm since initial presentation. Cause unclear. Patient reports feeling 'calmer than I should.'

-- Dr. Kit Lenford

---

**FOLLOW-UP:** Day 79
**CLASSIFICATION:** Restricted

Celebration. Mandatory. The memo said 'morale event.' The attendees said nothing.

Patient's neural scan shows anomalous activity in patterns that match REM sleep -- while patient is fully conscious. I've requested a second scanner. Request denied. Reason: 'equipment allocation insufficient for non-critical cases.'

This is not a non-critical case. But I don't have the vocabulary to explain why, because the vocabulary I'd need doesn't exist in the diagnostic manual.

Patient drew a diagram during evaluation. Unprompted. The diagram matches schematics for Eldritch Nodes that are classified above my clearance level. Patient has no access to classified materials. Patient says they 'just knew what it looked like.'

Storing original diagram in personal effects. Not filing with Mammona medical.

-- Dr. Kit Lenford

---

**UPDATE:** Day 89
**CLASSIFICATION:** Restricted / Protocol 7

Patient's condition reclassified under Protocol 7 ('Anomalous Presentation'). I was not informed that Protocol 7 files route directly to MasTema until after I filed.

Patient's bloodwork from today does not match patient's bloodwork from Day 66. Not in the way that bloodwork changes over time. In the way that it belongs to a different person. Same name. Same face. Different blood.

I have requested consultation with a specialist. Request forwarded to MasTema. No response.

-- Dr. Kit Lenford

---

**NOTE:** Day 106
**CLASSIFICATION:** Personal / Not Filed

I'm no longer confident this is a medical issue. I'm no longer confident in the word 'issue.' Patient is functional. More than functional. Outperforming baseline metrics across all categories. If this is a disease, it's improving them. That's not how disease works. That's not how anything works.

Unless the improvement is the disease. Unless what I'm calling improvement is something else wearing a familiar shape.

I don't know anymore. I'm filing this report because filing reports is what I do. I don't know what else to do.

-- Dr. Kit Lenford

---

**ADDENDUM** (handwritten, different hand):

Doctor Lenford's notes are thorough and accurate. The observations are described. What they mean is not.

What it means is that Quinn Burnside is no longer the patient. Quinn Burnside is the vector. The patient is everyone else.

Recommend immediate--

[The addendum ends here. The pen was pressed hard enough to score the surface of the datapad. The handwriting has not been matched to any personnel on the posting.]

---

**Cross-reference:** See historical record 'The Missing Report'

============================================================
### [SEQ:72] [Data Pad] [21:10:54]
============================================================

## DATA PAD: Maintenance Log -- Arjun Deepwell
**Found at:** Twin Sun Observatory | **Tone:** tender | **Type:** maintenance log

---

**MAINTENANCE LOG -- Atmospheric Processor Bay 1**
**Tech:** Arjun Deepwell (AD-675)
**Date:** Day 38

Routine inspection. All parameters within spec. Filter replacement completed. Calibration nominal. Estimated next service: Day 68.

Notes: None. Clean run.

-- AD-675

---

**Date:** Day 46

Noticed during routine walkthrough: the array has a new sound. Not a malfunction sound. Not in the diagnostic library. Low frequency. Intermittent. Occurs every 165 seconds. Can't identify source component.

Action: monitoring. Will revisit next scheduled maintenance.

-- AD-675

---

**Date:** Day 59

A flower had grown through a crack in the hydroponics bay. Nobody reported it.

Follow-up on previous entry. The deviation has recurred. Same magnitude. Same duration. Same time: 0300.

Submitted work order for diagnostic overhaul. Status: queued. Estimated wait: 5 weeks.

Something isn't right. I don't have data to support that statement. After 20 years of fixing machines, you develop a feeling in your hands. The feeling says something isn't right.

-- AD-675

---

**Date:** Day 77

Diagnostic overhaul completed. 5 hours of testing. Every component individually verified. Every sensor calibrated. Every connection checked.

Result: the system is operating perfectly. Better than perfectly. Efficiency is up 3% from installation baseline. That shouldn't be possible on equipment this age with this service history.

But here's the thing that keeps me up: during the overhaul, I found a component I didn't install. Didn't order. Can't identify. It's wired into the main bus. It's functioning. I don't know what it does. I tried to remove it. It's grown into the surrounding wiring. 'Grown' is the word. I'm using it deliberately.

I'm filing this under 'resolved -- nominal' because that's what the data says. The data is wrong. I don't know how to file that.

-- AD-675

---

**Date:** Day 81

She left a chocolate ration on his bunk. No note. None needed.

I found something behind the access panel that shouldn't be there. Not a component. A cavity. Small. The size of a fist. The interior surface is smooth in a way that machined metal isn't smooth. In a way that nothing manufactured is smooth. And there's something in it. Small. Warm. I didn't touch it.

I closed the panel. I'm not going to open it again.

I'm not going to file this.

-- AD-675

[No further entries in this log. Subsequent maintenance logs for Atmospheric Processor Bay 1 are filed by a different technician. They describe the system as 'nominal.']

---

**Cross-reference:** See historical record 'Dream Concordance Event'

### [SEQ:74] [Quest] [21:10:54]
============================================================

## QUEST: Broken Chain
**Genre:** Faction Tension | **Tone:** slow_dread / dissociation
**Location:** Scorched Wildlands | **Faction:** Silent Compact

**Trigger:**
The morning shift doesn't report. Twenty colonists in the mess hall. Arms crossed. Doors locked from the inside. Hyun Dewitt stands at the front. 'We're not going back down,' Hyun says. 'Not until you listen.'

**Setup:**
The cracks in the floor were spreading. Following the same pattern as the ruins. It's not anger. That's what's frightening about it. The colonists in the mess hall are calm. Resolved. They've thought about this. Hyun has a list of demands. The list is reasonable. The timing isn't. The bore shaft needs monitoring. The reactor needs hands. Every hour of shutdown is a degree colder.

She watched her hands work the controls. They knew what to do. She was somewhere else.

**Key NPC:** Hyun Dewitt (Mining: 9, Willpower: 3) -- their mining skill determines the approach. They motivation: stockpile enough supplies that the next crisis doesn't kill them.

**Objectives:**
1. Read the demands. They're not unreasonable: medical supplies, rotation schedule, hazard pay, and one thing you can't give them -- the truth about what's in the deep bore.
2. Negotiate without violence. The colonists are armed with tools. The security team is armed with worse. The gap between tools and weapons is smaller than anyone thinks.
3. Decide which demands are met, which are promised, and which are refused. The colony's survival depends on getting this right.

**Required Checks:**
- Social (8+, extreme) -- get the witness to talk
- Perception (6+, hard) -- spot the tripwire before it's too late
- Deception (5+, normal) -- pass as authorized personnel

**If Social succeeds:** Trust earned. The hard way. The only way that lasts.
**If Social fails:** The walls went up. Whatever trust existed just evaporated.
**If Perception succeeds:** There it is. The detail everyone missed. It changes the equation.
**If Perception fails:** Looked right at it. Didn't see it. The brain filtered it out as normal.
**If Deception succeeds:** They bought it. Every word. The performance was flawless.
**If Deception fails:** They saw through it. Not the lie itself -- the performance. Now they're watching.

**Conditions:**
- Ice fog (visibility 30%, Perception at disadvantage)
- Supply window (limited time before the shuttle departs)
- Reactor at 80% (brownouts in outer sections, Repair checks at -10)

**The Choice:**
Talk to Hyun alone. Leader to leader. Find out what Hyun really wants. The list is a negotiating position. The real ask is underneath.
OR
Break the mutiny. Security. Force. Arrests. The colony works tomorrow. The colony hates you forever. Pick one.

**Twist:**
The demands include something buried in the fine print: access to the colony's escape shuttles. Not everyone. Just the families. Hyun knows something is coming. Something that makes the bore shaft irrelevant.

**Faction Stakes:**
- Silent Compact wants the evidence destroyed. Compliance: +reputation, -22 thermal cores as 'bonus'
- Vanguard Alliance wants Hyun Dewitt extracted. Compliance: you lose a colonist. They gain an asset.
- The colonists don't know about this yet. When they find out, morale takes the hit regardless of outcome.

**Consequences (if Choice A):**
- Section A reopened. What's inside changes the colony's understanding of the site.
- Hyun Dewitt gains colony trust. They knows things they didn't before.
- Morale drops for a week. Then stabilizes. The new normal is lower than the old one.

**Consequences (if Choice B):**
- The situation is contained. On paper. The nightmares aren't on paper.
- Hyun Dewitt requests reassignment. The request sits in a queue that doesn't move.
- Greta Varga starts asking questions. The questions are pointed.

**Outcomes by Roll:**
- Critical success: Both objectives achieved without cost. The colony gains leverage and resources.
- Success: Done. Not clean, not elegant, but done. The colony moves forward.
- Failure: Objective failed, but information gained. The failure teaches more than success would have.
- Critical failure: Total loss. The situation is worse than before the attempt. And now they know you tried.

**Dialogue During Quest:**
- Hyun: "HERMES has been routing power to the comms array. Nobody asked it to."
- Player: [Respond / Stay Silent / Leave]
- Hyun: "Don't leave me here. Not in the dark."

**Reward:** 12 thermal cores, an ally inside MasTema
