### [SEQ:268] [History Event] [21:11:14]
============================================================

## HISTORY: The Listening Post
**Era:** corporate | **Tone:** gallows_humor

Orientation manual, page one: 'Welcome. You've made a terrible decision.'

In the first week of the previous posting, the HERMES terminal in Section B displayed a message: 'WELCOME BACK.' The posting was new. Nobody had been welcomed back. HERMES has no record of the message.

**Significance:** The previous crew knew something. The question is whether their silence was chosen or enforced.

**Mechanical Impact:**
- Colony morale: -10 for one week (the news spreads)
- the deep bore access corridor: sealed (inaccessible until quest completes)
- NPC Naomi Dustborn: arc shifts to 'defiant'
- Fortune Arms & Munitions sends a coded transmission within hours. They shouldn't know about this yet.

**Evidence:**
- Maintenance log #6800 (references a 'routine personnel transfer' on the same date)
- Scorch marks on the corridor wall of Section B (painted over, visible under UV)
- A photograph found in Rolf Norris's quarters -- shows a room that doesn't exist in the colony
- HERMES has the footage. HERMES won't share the footage.

**Who Knows:**
- **Marisol Morales:** was on shift when it happened. Says nothing unusual occurred. Their hands shake when they say it.
- **Sigrid Chen:** wasn't there but knows someone who was. That someone left the colony two weeks later.
- **Dev Caro:** arrived the week after. Wasn't briefed. But the bunk they were assigned to still smells like smoke.

**Aftermath:**
- A memorial was proposed by the colonists. Mammona denied it. The denial cited 'insufficient evidence of loss.'
- Fortune Arms & Munitions made inquiries. The inquiries were careful. Indirect. The kind of careful that means they already know.
- Rolf Norris hasn't been the same since. The change is subtle -- quieter meals, fewer questions, early shifts.

**HERMES Analysis:**
HERMES maintains a shadow log of the event. The shadow log contains data that contradicts the official record. Both records are internally consistent. Both cannot be true.

**Related Incidents:**
A similar event was recorded at Flooded Wing C in 2553. The similarities were noted by one analyst. The analyst was reassigned.

**Investigation Check:**
- Deception (8+, extreme) -- dig deeper into what happened
- **If successful:** the evidence leads to a sealed drive in the maintenance crawlspace. The drive is still warm.
- **If failed:** the trail goes cold. Not because the evidence is gone. Because someone cleaned it. Recently.

**Social Mask:** Presents as the loyalist to a person. Actually: the loyalty isn't to the cause. It's to one person who showed them kindness when nobody else would. The cause is incidental. Tell: follows the person's lead regardless of whether the decision makes sense. The loyalty is visible. The reason isn't.

**Background:**
Two years in a Mammona labor camp on Hollowhold taught Wren everything about survival and nothing about hope.

Wren's right arm hasn't felt right since shrapnel from a detonation charge set too early. Phantom pain. Except the limb is still there.

She traded her last personal item for a membrane pressed between glass slides -- it still contracts. Says it was worth it. Won't make eye contact when she says it.

A sealed envelope in Wren's bunk contains instructions. To be opened on a specific date. The date is six weeks from now.

She'd stopped saying 'I' three weeks ago. 'We' was the only pronoun that felt real.

**What Drives Them:** botany. Keeps a single plant alive in a jar. Talks to it. Moved it twice to catch better light. The light hasn't changed.
**What Haunts Them:** her own hands. What they did with them on the last posting. What they might do again if pushed.

**Dialogue:**
- "How long's your contract? Doesn't matter. You'll be here longer."
- "Don't walk the corridors alone past 0200. That's not policy. That's experience."
- "I recognized something in the bore shaft. A sound. From a dream I had when I was nine. I've never told anyone about the dream."
- "The perimeter lights keep cutting out. Same section. Every night. Maintenance replaced the bulbs. Same section. Same time."
- "Someone found something in the deep bore. They won't say what."
- "I used to have a dog. On Rhea-2. She'd sleep on my feet."

**Connections:**
**Connection:** witnessed the death of Linnea Altman
**Love:** had someone waiting back on Orbit Hub 71. Found out they didn't wait. Found out by letter.

**Physical:** always cold. Even when everyone else is sweating. Holds mugs with both hands.
**Habit:** keeps a piece of thermal core in her pocket. Says it keeps her warm. The core is cold.
**Debt:** ran a voidbloom operation out of a supply cache. The customers remember.

**Sample Check:** Building (8) vs easy: rolled 78/95 -- success
functional. Not pretty, but it works. Good enough for Erebus.

**Quest Hook:**
Every third shift, Wren disappears for two hours. She comes back smelling like copper and ozone.

Credits: -10,317 | Monthly: 7,816 (after Mammona deductions: ~1,328)
inherited someone else's debt. A co-signer who died. A family member who defaulted. The creditor doesn't care whose name was first on the form.

**Identity:**
built for endurance, not speed. Wide shoulders, short legs, center of gravity low enough to work in wind. looks younger than they're. People underestimate them. They've learned to use that. half-sibling they didn't know about contacted them last year. From Nerthus-9. Wants to meet.

**Motivation:** keep their head down and avoid being noticed by anyone with authority
**Hidden Agenda:** watching the bore shaft readings and correlating them with seismic data from the perimeter sensors. The correlation shows something moving. They haven't told anyone.
**Social Mask:** None -- what you see is close to what you get

**Background:**
Court records on Relay Station Nine gave Aven a choice: Thalassa Deep or an outer rim contract. They chose the contract. Some days they wonder.

Lost their forearm to frostbite during an EVA that should've been cancelled. Filed the complaint. Nothing changed.

Eats alone. Not by policy. By preference. The mess hall clears a radius around them without being asked.

Aven knows that the last supply drop contained items that weren't on any requisition form. Found out by accident. Has been sleeping with one eye open since.

Rust on the drill platform smelled like the swing set in the park behind his mother's apartment. Iron and rain.

the colony air recyclers handle the big stuff. Dust, fibers, anything you can see. The small particulates — the ones from bore processing, from wall off-gassing, from the thermal core refinery — pass through. Everyone breathes them. Everyone coughs. The cough is dry, persistent, and universal. By month three it's background noise. Mammona medical logs it as 'minor respiratory acclimatization.' The acclimatization involves microscopic scarring of the alveoli. The scarring is permanent.

**What Drives Them:** reading. Has one book. Has read it forty times. Could recite it blind. Different passages mean different things each time.
**What Haunts Them:** the comms going silent. Not static -- nothing. Like the relay stopped existing. Like the rest of the galaxy stopped.

**Dialogue:**
- "Don't get comfortable. Nobody does."
- "You hear singing, you come back. Don't follow it."
- "I did something. On my last posting. I can't undo it."
- "Drill output's down twelve percent this week. Same crew. Same equipment. Something's different underground."
- "I heard Mammona's been here before. We're not the first team."
- "Touch that again and you'll lose the hand."

**Connections:**
**Connection:** betrayed Sigrid Chen
**Love:** shares a bunk with someone. Neither calls it what it's. Neither wants to jinx it.

**Physical:** old frostbite damage on the ears and nose. Tissue is numb. Doesn't feel the cold there anymore.
**Habit:** carries a dead comm unit. Checks it for messages twice a day. Morning and evening. Has been dead for months.
**Debt:** killed someone in self-defense. The investigation disagrees.

progress. Incremental. Another data point in the right direction.

============================================================
### [SEQ:274] [History Event] [21:11:15]
============================================================

## HISTORY: Roster Disappearance
**Era:** corporate | **Tone:** numb

The drill hit something. They noted it and kept drilling.

Paxtera AgroTech deployed a listening post at Signal Tower Erebus in 2550. The post transmitted for 3 weeks, then switched to a frequency not in Mammona's codebook. The transmissions continued for 3 months after the post was officially decommissioned.

**Significance:** This wasn't documented. Undocumented events on a Mammona posting aren't oversights. They're policy.

**Mechanical Impact:**
- Colony morale: -3 for one week (the news spreads)
- the deep bore access corridor: sealed (inaccessible until quest completes)
- NPC Tariq Wallace: arc shifts to 'compromised'
- HERMES begins logging increased activity in the area. The logs are not shared.

**Evidence:**
- Maintenance log #9190 (references a 'routine personnel transfer' on the same date)
- Tool marks on the sealed door -- someone tried to open it. From the inside.
- A letter addressed to no one. The handwriting matches two different people.
- HERMES has the footage. HERMES won't share the footage.

**Who Knows:**
- **Lev Caro:** was on shift when it happened. Says nothing unusual occurred. Their hands shake when they say it.
- **Elena Oduya:** pieced together a timeline from maintenance logs. The timeline has a gap. The gap is shaped like a decision.
- **Helmut Choi:** has no connection to the event. But they've been dreaming about it. Accurately.

**Aftermath:**
- The supply manifest format changed. The new format has a field labeled 'ANOMALY FLAG.' It is always set to NO.
- A sealed communication was sent to MasTema. Response: 'Acknowledged.' No follow-up. No action. Just acknowledgment.
- Someone started leaving offerings near the site. Small things -- food, water, a folded note. Nobody claims responsibility.

**HERMES Analysis:**
HERMES generated a 16-page analysis. The analysis was compressed to a single paragraph for the colony briefing. The paragraph says: 'No anomalies detected.' The 25 pages say otherwise.

**Related Incidents:**
Three postings. Three events. Three sealed reports. The reports use the same classification code. The code isn't in any manual.

**Investigation Check:**
- Deception (5+, normal) -- dig deeper into what happened
- **If successful:** the evidence leads to a sealed drive in the maintenance crawlspace. The drive is still warm.
- **If failed:** the investigation alerts someone. Within hours, the remaining evidence begins to disappear.

lanky and awkward on flat ground. Climbs like something that evolved for vertical surfaces. metabolism runs hot. Sweats when everyone else is shivering. Eats twice the rations. orphaned by the Fall of Fortuna. Raised in Mammona's youth program. Knows nothing else.

**Hidden Agenda:** None apparent
**Social Mask:** None -- what you see is close to what you get

Worked as a skinwalker tracker on three different postings before Mammona consolidated them all under one contract. Adaeze's contract. Adaeze wasn't consulted.

A contamination alarm on Ironreach locked Adaeze in a decon chamber for seventy-two hours. Alone. In the dark.

Adaeze keeps a tally of shifts worked since arriving. Scratched into the bunk frame. Four hundred and counting.

MasTema has a file on Adaeze. Thin. But it exists. And someone keeps adding to it.

She promised herself one more year. Same promise she'd made last year.

**What Drives Them:** tattoos. Does them by hand with a needle and salvaged ink. Each one a story. Each story has to be true. That's the rule.
**What Haunts Them:** HERMES. The way it pauses before answering. Like it's choosing between truths.

**Dialogue:**

- "My contract says 'hazard pay.' The hazard is the pay."

**Connections:**
**Connection:** ex partner of Dev Caro
**Love:** sends birthday gifts through the supply chain. Takes three months to arrive. Sends them four months early. The math is careful.

**Physical:** skin slightly too tight across the cheekbones. Like it was stretched. Or like something underneath is pressing out.
**Habit:** carries a smokestick behind her ear but never lights it. Replaces it when it crumbles. Same brand every time.
**Debt:** embezzled M-Points by exploiting a payroll glitch. Mammona fixed the glitch. They haven't fixed the thief.

**Health:** chronic dental abscess: swollen jaw, right side. The colony doesn't have a dentist. The medic drained it once. It came back. Eats on the left side. Doesn't smile wide.

During StarByte's crew went into cryostasis as the sector collapsed, Diego made a choice about who to save. Made the wrong one. Knows it every morning.

Knows every corridor, every vent, every locked door in the colony. Mapped them in the first week.

Diego has been mapping the colony's blind spots. Camera gaps. Patrol timing. Not for escape. For something else.

The lights cut. When they came back, someone was standing in the corridor who hadn't been there.

**Health:** precursor material lung (berylliosis analog): granulomas in the lungs from inhaling precursor ruin dust. The ancient stone isn't stone — it's a composite that the human immune system attacks on contact. Chronic cough, weight loss, night sweats. The ruin mappers and specimen handlers get it first. Mammona issues standard respirators. The particles are smaller than standard respirators catch.

can't go to the infirmary. Won't let the medic touch them. Self-treats cuts with a stolen kit in his bunk. The stitches are uneven but they hold. avoidance. Complete, thorough avoidance. Hasn't had a checkup in fourteen months.

### [SEQ:279] [History Event] [21:11:15]
============================================================

## HISTORY: The Surplus Drops
**Era:** fortuna | **Tone:** resigned

Tomorrow will be the same. This is the job.

Someone carved a map into the floor of Section E. The map shows tunnels that don't exist. Or didn't exist when the map was carved. Three of the seven tunnels have since been discovered by the drill team.

**Significance:** Whatever happened here before your arrival wasn't an accident. It was a sequence.

**Mechanical Impact:**
- Colony morale: -3 for until resolved (the news spreads)
- the maintenance tunnels: sealed (inaccessible until quest completes)
- NPC Marisol Morales: arc shifts to 'compromised'
- HERMES reclassifies the location. The new classification is above colony clearance.

**Evidence:**
- Maintenance log #3043 (references nothing. The entry for that date is blank. Every other entry is filled.)
- Residue on the ventilation grate -- organic, unidentified, warm to the touch
- A sketch of the bore shaft showing a passage that isn't on any schematic. The sketch is accurate.
- HERMES's sensor data shows a temperature spike in the affected area. Duration: 0.3 seconds. Magnitude: +200C.

**Who Knows:**
- **Lark Caro:** was the first to arrive at the scene. What they saw doesn't match the official account.
- **Dante MacReady:** heard about it secondhand. Has theories. The theories are wrong but close.
- **Anya MacReady:** has no connection to the event. But they've been dreaming about it. Accurately.

**Aftermath:**
- A memorial was proposed by the colonists. Mammona denied it. The denial cited 'insufficient evidence of loss.'
- Two faction operatives were seen near Voss Landing the following week. They didn't enter. They measured something.
- The night shift in the affected area has had four different crews in two months. Nobody asks for a second rotation.

**HERMES Analysis:**
HERMES generated a 41-page analysis. The analysis was compressed to a single paragraph for the colony briefing. The paragraph says: 'No anomalies detected.' The 33 pages say otherwise.

**Related Incidents:**
Three postings. Three events. Three sealed reports. The reports use the same classification code. The code isn't in any manual.

**Investigation Check:**
- Research (6+, hard) -- dig deeper into what happened
- **If successful:** the evidence leads to a sealed drive in the maintenance crawlspace. The drive is still warm.
- **If failed:** nothing. But 'nothing' on a Mammona posting is never nothing. It's a redaction.

aging body that hasn't caught up with the mind. Still moves like she's thirty. Pays for it at night. tall enough to hit her head on standard bulkhead frames. Does it twice a day minimum. raised by her older brother after her parents went into debt servitude. The brother is still in debt. She send money. It's never enough.

**Motivation:** build something that outlasts the contract. A system, a structure, a legacy
**Hidden Agenda:** copying data from every terminal they access. Building a file. The file is for someone outside the colony.
**Social Mask:** None -- what you see is close to what you get

**Background:**
A salvage diver by training, Jessa ended up on Erebus because Rust Meridian needed someone expendable and she needed to disappear.

Watched a friend walk onto the ice and not come back. Stood at the perimeter for six hours waiting. The cold took two toes.

Runs the unofficial book on colony events. Who's betting what. Who owes whom. The ledger never lies.

Jessa has been mapping the colony's blind spots. Camera gaps. Patrol timing. Not for escape. For something else.

She counted the footsteps behind her. They matched hers. Exactly.

thermal core extraction without proper shielding. The cores emit a radiation spectrum that standard gear doesn't block. The burns present as deep tissue damage — skin looks intact on the surface, but underneath the cells are dying. The tissue changes. Doesn't heal normally. Grows back different — thicker, darker, with a texture that's not quite skin. The extraction teams call it core burn. The scar tissue is warm to the touch. Always warm. Even on Erebus.

**What Drives Them:** silence. Seeks it out. Hoards it. Maps the quiet spots in the colony the way others map supply caches.
**What Haunts Them:** her own hands. What they did with them on the last posting. What they might do again if pushed.

**Dialogue:**
- "You want to know the rules? There's one: survive your shift."
- "Don't eat anything you didn't bring with you."
- "I know what HERMES does at night. When we're all asleep. I checked the logs. I wish I hadn't."
- "Someone stacked thermal cores outside the mess hall door last night. In a pattern. Nobody took credit."
- "Two colonists were talking about the bore shaft at 0200. When I got closer, they stopped. Not like they paused -- like someone hit mute."
- "My contract says 'hazard pay.' The hazard is the pay."
- "Guy on my last crew said he could fix anything. Reactor, drill, comms array. Couldn't fix his marriage though. Or the hull breach that killed him."

**Connections:**
**Connection:** sibling of Cross Crestfall
**Love:** promised to come back for someone. The promise is three years old. The shuttle costs haven't changed.

**Physical:** missing an ear. Replaced with a metal cap bolted to the skull. Hears fine. Hears more than fine.
**Habit:** names every piece of equipment. Gets upset when others don't use the right name. The drill is Margaret.
**Debt:** smuggled refugees past a UTC checkpoint. Got caught. Let go. Owes someone for the 'let go' part.

**Sample Check:** Hunting (10) vs easy: rolled 83/95 -- success
survived. Landed hits. Took some. The math worked out.

Jessa received a package from Karnaith Transit Docks. No return address. Inside: a Mammona-branded pen that records audio when clicked and a note that says 'You know what to do.'

**Stats:**

STR: 4 | END: 7 | AGI: 8 | PER: 8 | INT: 6 | CHA: 7 | WIL: 6 | EMP: 7

**Economic:**
Credits: 21,533 | Monthly: 1,495 (after Mammona deductions: ~254)
collected on a workplace injury claim. The injury was real. The recovery was quicker than the paperwork suggested. Mammona's insurance arm paid out and flagged the file.

**Identity:**
thin in a way that suggests metabolism, not hunger. Eats constantly. Burns it off by existing. long fingers. Surgeon's hands, their mother called them. Uses them for wiring instead. family scattered across five postings. They share a group message. It updates once a month. The delays make every message feel like a letter from the past.

**Motivation:** nothing matters. The colony will fail. Mammona will win. The ice will take everything. In the meantime, they watch (hidden)
**Hidden Agenda:** None apparent
**Social Mask:** None -- what you see is close to what you get

**Background:**
The welder guild on Bonerest expelled Sage after they dug too deep and found something alive beneath Foras. Mammona doesn't care about guild standings. Mammona cares about warm bodies.

The thing Sage found in the deep bore on Bonerest wasn't dead. It moved when they touched it. Nobody believed them.

Sage fixed the water recycler when three qualified techs couldn't. Didn't ask for credit. Didn't get any.

Sage received a transmission meant for someone else. The content described HERMES in terms that don't match any public record.

The memo was seven pages long. The word 'death' didn't appear. The word 'attrition' appeared nine times.

**What Drives Them:** weather. Tracks barometric changes by feel. Better than the instruments, most days. Predicts storms by the pain in their knees.
**What Haunts Them:** fire. Not the flames. The sound of things burning. Holds their breath when someone strikes a match.

**Dialogue:**
- "Keep your head down the first week. After that, keep it down permanently."
- "Don't go near the bore shaft after dark."
- "I'm not as good at this job as they think. Been faking competence for six months. Nobody's died yet. Yet."
- "Haven't seen the night shift crew in two days. Their bunks are made. Gear's still in the lockers."
- "One of the engineers found old Mammona equipment. Decades old. Down there."
- "What do you call an optimist on Erebus? New."
- "Please. I know I don't deserve it. Please."

**Connections:**
**Connection:** trusts Jessa Thorne
**Love:** writes letters to someone who stopped writing back three months ago.

**Physical:** a tooth that's a different color from the rest. Replaced after a fight. Wrong shade. Obvious when they smile.
**Habit:** talks to a photograph before every shift. Doesn't say what. The photograph faces the wall.
**Debt:** sold information to the Void Serpents once. Once was enough.

**Health:** chronic gastritis: can't eat on an empty stomach. Can't eat on a full stomach. Has worked out a schedule. Eight small meals. The mess hall runs three sittings.

**Sample Check:** Building (8) vs easy: rolled 29/95 -- strong success
solid repair. Will hold. Properly done, properly sealed.

**Quest Hook:**
Sage has been tracking the generator's power output. The numbers don't match the fuel consumption. Something else is drawing power.

Mining: 2 | Building: 7 | Cooking: 6 | Hunting: 9 | Research: 2 | Medical: 7
STR: 4 | END: 4 | AGI: 4 | PER: 6 | INT: 5 | CHA: 8 | WIL: 4 | EMP: 3

**Economic:**
Credits: 4,136 | Monthly: 2,834 (after Mammona deductions: ~481)
manages the budget. Sends money home when the comms work. Has a small reserve for emergencies. The reserve shrinks every month.

**Identity:**
scarred across the torso from an industrial accident. The skin pulled tight when it healed. Moves stiff on cold mornings. Loosens up by noon. sharp features, angular. Looks carved out of something. Resting expression reads as hostile. twin. The other twin is on Rhea-2. He used to feel each other's pain. Now it's just absence.

**Motivation:** earn back a name they destroyed. The name belongs to their family (hidden)
**Hidden Agenda:** None apparent
**Social Mask:** None -- what you see is close to what you get

**Background:**
Nikolai Mbeki used to teach. frost line surveyor certification, Hyades technical institute. The institute closed. Mammona was hiring. Teaching and mining have one thing in common: you dig.

A Mammona psych evaluation flagged Nikolai for 'abnormal stress response.' He responded by punching the evaluator. The flag was upgraded.

Between shifts, Nikolai can be found near the perimeter. Watching. Not the ice. The door. Always the door.

Nikolai knows that the Skinwalkers aren't native to Erebus. They were brought here. This knowledge is the most dangerous thing he owns.

The radio crackled. Behind the static, something that might have been a voice. Or a heartbeat.

grips railings like he's load-bearing promises. Won't look down ladders. Gets through the day by staring at fixed points.

**What Drives Them:** soil. Collects it from supply crates, from boots, from packing material. Keeps samples labeled. Smells them. Says each planet's dirt is different.
**What Haunts Them:** the blood test. Not the needle. Not the results. The pause before the results.

**Dialogue:**
- "With respect, don't touch anything marked red. Don't ask why it's marked red."
- "The thermal cores are warm. That's normal. If they're hot, drop it and run."
- "I understand, but i haven't slept properly in weeks. Not insomnia. I hear things when I close my eyes. Coordinates. Repeated."
- "I understand, but the supply cache is lighter than it should be. Counted twice. Short by eleven ration packs and a medkit."
- "With respect, night shift says they heard drilling. From below the deepest shaft."
- "With respect, my bunk's got condensation running down the wall. Again."

**Connections:**
**Connection:** partner of Cross Crestfall
**Love:** grieving someone who isn't dead. Just gone. Just unreachable.

**Physical:** skin grafts on both forearms. Different texture, slightly different shade. Keeps them covered indoors.
**Habit:** sketches the same building over and over. Different angles. Same building. It doesn't exist. They've never seen it.
**Debt:** was bailed out of Eclipse's End by a Dread Corsair captain. The bill is outstanding.

**Sample Check:** Hunting (9) vs hard: rolled 62/75 -- success
survived. Landed hits. Took some. The math worked out.

**Quest Hook:**
Nikolai is being stalked. Not by colony fauna. By another colonist. He knows who. Can't prove it.

## NPC: Mei Nichols
**Gender:** Female | **Age:** 57 | **Occupation:** salvager
**Traits:** Quick Thinker, Reckless
**Faction:** Fallen Threshold | **Arc Stage:** desperate | **Archetype:** rebel
**Tone:** desperate

**Stats:**
Mining: 3 | Building: 6 | Cooking: 2 | Hunting: 3 | Research: 8 | Medical: 5
STR: 4 | END: 5 | AGI: 4 | PER: 2 | INT: 4 | CHA: 3 | WIL: 3 | EMP: 4

**Economic:**
Credits: 30 | Monthly: 1,983 (after Mammona deductions: ~337)
arrived with nothing. Owns what Mammona issued: one uniform, one blanket, one set of utensils. Everything else is borrowed or stolen.

**Identity:**
barrel-chested with arms that don't hang straight. Too much muscle in the way. Useful in a mine. Useless in a crawlspace. prematurely grey. Started at nineteen. Genetic. The colony thinks it's stress. father was a drunk and a driller. Good at one of those things. Taught both.

**Motivation:** build something that outlasts the contract. A system, a structure, a legacy
**Hidden Agenda:** None apparent
**Social Mask:** Presents as the devout. Actually: uses faith as a framework for control. Belief optional. Tell: the prayers are public. The private hours are spent on something else entirely.

**Background:**
Personnel file says Mei transferred from AgroBlock 14 voluntarily. Personnel files say a lot of things. Mei says nothing.

Mei doesn't talk about the 6 years between AgroBlock 14 and Erebus. The silence says enough.

Sends half of every pay cycle to AgroBlock 14. Won't say who it goes to. The other half goes to the debt.

The signal Mei picked up last week matches a frequency she heard on AgroBlock 14. It shouldn't exist in two places.

Blood on the wall. Still wet. The body was gone.

gaps. Minutes, sometimes hours. Comes back mid-sentence, mid-task, mid-step. Doesn't always know it happened. Others do. writes timestamps on her hand. Checks them. The gaps between timestamps tell the story.

**What Drives Them:** cards. Not gambling -- the game itself. Knows every variant from every posting. Invented two of her own. One is actually good.
**What Haunts Them:** waking up and not knowing where they're. Happens more often now. Takes longer to remember.

**Dialogue:**
- "Another warm body. Good. We're short-handed."
- "If the ground shakes, don't freeze. Run toward the colony, not away."
- "I've been marking the walls. Small marks. Low, where nobody looks. I don't know why. My hands do it while I'm thinking about something else."
- "The colony dogs are sleeping in shifts now. One always watches the south wall. They organized this on their own."
- "Quartermaster's been marking inventory down. Not up. Down. Someone's skimming."
- "I don't know who I'm talking to. Doesn't matter. Just let the shuttle come."
- "There's still time. There has to be."

**Connections:**
**Connection:** nemesis of Dalia Petrov
**Love:** married to the job. Not a metaphor. The contract literally owns her time.

**Physical:** a patch of skin on the forearm that is always warm. Medical can't explain it. She rest cold hands on it.
**Habit:** talks to the reactor when nobody's watching. Hand flat on the housing. Claims it talks back. Nobody laughs.
**Debt:** burned a Dustweaver drone. The Swarm logged the perpetrator. Retaliation is pending.

**Health:** filter cough (particulate accumulation): the colony air recyclers handle the big stuff. Dust, fibers, anything you can see. The small particulates — the ones from bore processing, from wall off-gassing, from the thermal core refinery — pass through. Everyone breathes them. Everyone coughs. The cough is dry, persistent, and universal. By month three it's background noise. Mammona medical logs it as 'minor respiratory acclimatization.' The acclimatization involves microscopic scarring of the alveoli. The scarring is permanent.

**Sample Check:** Research (8) vs normal: rolled 71/80 -- success
progress. Incremental. Another data point in the right direction.

**Quest Hook:**
A colonist Mei worked with on Hollowpoint Relay arrived on the last shuttle. They won't make eye contact.

## NPC: Ash Dvorak
**Gender:** Non-binary | **Age:** 27 | **Occupation:** animal handler
**Traits:** Resourceful, Night Terrors
**Faction:** MasTema Incorporated | **Archetype:** survivor
**Tone:** grief

**Stats:**
Mining: 1 | Building: 3 | Cooking: 10 | Hunting: 5 | Research: 1 | Medical: 5
STR: 5 | END: 4 | AGI: 3 | PER: 5 | INT: 8 | CHA: 4 | WIL: 6 | EMP: 6

**Economic:**
Credits: 612 | Monthly: 2,880 (after Mammona deductions: ~489)
earns enough to survive. Nothing left over. Can't afford passage off-planet. Can't afford to get sick. Can't afford anything that isn't survival.

**Identity:**
tall enough to bang their head on every hatch on the colony. Has a permanent mark on the forehead. thick eyebrows that move independently when they're thinking. Expressive. Betrays every thought. mother died in a Mammona facility. Official cause: equipment failure. Unofficial cause: budget cuts. Carries the incident report in their bag.

**Motivation:** get through the contract alive
**Hidden Agenda:** None apparent
**Social Mask:** Presents as the complainer. Actually: deeply invested. Complains because they care. If they stopped complaining, they'd have stopped caring. The colony can't afford that. Tell: the complaints are specific and actionable. They're filed in order of severity. That's not complaining. That's reporting.

**Background:**
On Skinwalker Ridge, they called Ash Dvorak a survivor. They flinch at the word.

Ash was on shift when the containment failed. The colleague next to them didn't make it. Ash was wearing their gear.

The bunkmates say Ash cries in their sleep. Ash says they don't dream. Both things might be true.

Someone on the colony is reporting to MasTema Incorporated. Ash knows because they's the one doing it.

An empty chair at the table. Everyone noticed. No one moved it.

**What Drives Them:** fermentation. Brews something from stolen ration ingredients. It's terrible. Burns the throat. Everyone asks for more.
**What Haunts Them:** the comms going silent. Not static -- nothing. Like the relay stopped existing. Like the rest of the galaxy stopped.

**Dialogue:**
- "Fresh contract? Yeah, I can tell by the eyes."
- "Stay inside the perimeter. I'm not asking."
- "I forged my qualification papers. The real me couldn't wire a junction box. I learned on the job. Mostly."
- "The thermal cores we pulled this morning were heavier than last week's. Same depth. Same vein."
- "They found NutriLoaf wrappers in the waste tunnel. Nobody's supposed to be down there."
- "Don't send me out there alone. I'm asking you as a person, not a contract number."
- "I'm not a believer. But if something's up there, now would be a good time."

**Connections:**
**Connection:** killed Sigrid Chen
**Love:** involved with someone dangerous. Knows it's dangerous. That might be part of it.

**Physical:** one arm visibly shorter than the other. Grew back wrong after the accident. Compensates without thinking.
**Habit:** keeps a small notebook. Writes one sentence per day. Won't show anyone. The cover says MAINTENANCE LOG.
**Debt:** was sponsored by MasTema for training. The sponsorship comes with obligations. Indefinite ones.

**Sample Check:** Cooking (10) vs easy: rolled 70/95 -- success
survived. Hungry, cold, tired. But alive. That's the only metric that counts.

**Quest Hook:**
Ash is willing to share the location of a hidden cache at Sector 14. In exchange: help getting off Erebus.

arrived with nothing. Owns what Mammona issued: one uniform, one blanket, one set of utensils. Everything else is borrowed or stolen.

**Identity:**
gaunt since the supply shortage. Used to be heavier. The clothes don't fit right anymore. albino. The cold and the dark suit them better than the sun ever did. last surviving member of a crew that went into the ruins. Family by bond, not blood.

**Motivation:** destroy the reputation of someone who destroyed theirs. From the inside (hidden)
**Hidden Agenda:** None apparent
**Social Mask:** None -- what you see is close to what you get

**Background:**
Raised by a contract auditor on Cairnvale, Callista can strip a generator blindfolded but never learned to read until age nineteen.

What happened on Cairnvale is classified. Callista's nightmares aren't.

Likes: silence, black coffee, being left alone. Dislikes: questions about Cairnvale. About her hands. About the scar.

Before the posting, Dread Corsairs approached Callista with an offer. She hasn't accepted. She hasn't refused.

The wound healed too fast. What grew back wasn't the same tissue.

brilliant in bursts. Unreliable in stretches. Started six projects. Finished two. The two were exceptional. caffeine. Structure imposed by others. Falls apart during downtime.

**What Drives Them:** soil. Collects it from supply crates, from boots, from packing material. Keeps samples labeled. Smells them. Says each planet's dirt is different.
**What Haunts Them:** mirrors. Not superstition. The reflection does the same things. But a fraction of a second late.

**Dialogue:**
- "How long's your contract? Doesn't matter. You'll be here longer."
- "Don't walk the corridors alone past 0200. That's not policy. That's experience."
- "I recognized something in the bore shaft. A sound. From a dream I had when I was nine. I've never told anyone about the dream."
- "Haven't seen the night shift crew in two days. Their bunks are made. Gear's still in the lockers."
- "One of the engineers found old Mammona equipment. Decades old. Down there."
- "I'll pay it back. Every credit. Just give me time."

**Connections:**
**Connection:** co-conspirator with Briar Schmidt
**Love:** has a name tattooed on her ribs. Covered it with another tattoo. The first name is still visible if you look.

**Physical:** moves her mouth slightly before speaking. Like rehearsing. Or translating.
**Habit:** hums a lullaby when the generator cycles. Same melody. Same key. The generator cycles a lot.
**Debt:** was released from Thalassa Deep on condition of service. The condition is permanent.

**Mental Health:** trichotillomania — pulls hair when stressed. Eyebrows first, then scalp. Wears a beanie. The beanie hides it. Mostly. keeps hands busy. Always holding something. A pen, a bolt, a stone. When the hands are empty, they go to the hair.

**Sample Check:** Research (9) vs hard: rolled 98/75 -- critical failure
the anomaly in the data isn't an error. The anomaly is the answer. Nobody's going to like the answer.

**Quest Hook:**
Callista noticed that the bore shaft temperature spikes every 72 hours. Exactly. Nobody else has put the pattern together.

**Economic:**
Credits: 5,309 | Monthly: 2,574 (after Mammona deductions: ~437)
manages the budget. Sends money home when the comms work. Has a small reserve for emergencies. The reserve shrinks every month.

**Identity:**
tall enough to bang his head on every hatch on the colony. Has a permanent mark on the forehead. small and quick. Fits in crawlspaces nobody else can reach. Invaluable and knows it. raised by an aunt after parents were conscripted. The aunt stopped writing last year.

**Motivation:** disappear. New name, new posting, new life. The old one is too damaged (hidden)
**Hidden Agenda:** None apparent
**Social Mask:** Presents as the peacemaker. Actually: terrified of conflict because of what happened last time they were in one. Tell: mediates before being asked. Inserts themselves into arguments that don't concern them.

**Background:**
The letter of recommendation from Skinwalker Ridge describes Cole as 'competent under duress.' One sentence. No signature.

a contamination exposure nobody warned them about took his hearing in the left ear. Now he hears things nobody else does.

Cole writes letters that he never sends. Folds them into tight squares. The drawer under his bunk is full of tight squares.

Cole knows that Paxtera AgroTech's soil remediation program is a cover for burial operations. Found out by accident. Has been sleeping with one eye open since.

The employee of the month board had the same name for fourteen months straight. The employee was deceased.

**What Drives Them:** drawing. Fills notebooks with faces, machines, maps. Some of the maps show places nobody's been. Some of the faces belong to people nobody remembers.
**What Haunts Them:** HERMES. The way it pauses before answering. Like it's choosing between truths.

**Dialogue:**
- "You're new. You'll learn."
- "Don't walk the corridors alone past 0200. That's not policy. That's experience."
- "I haven't slept properly in weeks. Not insomnia. I hear things when I close my eyes. Coordinates. Repeated."
- "Generator's running hot. Been running hot all week. Sounds different too -- higher pitch, like it's working harder on something nobody asked for."
- "They found NutriLoaf wrappers in the waste tunnel. Nobody's supposed to be down there."
- "Last birthday I celebrated was on the transit ship. Someone found a candle. Real wax. We passed it around."

**Connections:**
**Connection:** widowed by Sage Nichols
**Love:** married. Spouse on Greenwall Settlement. Comms lag makes conversations a day old. He pretend it's real-time.

**Physical:** a tooth that's a different color from the rest. Replaced after a fight. Wrong shade. Obvious when they smile.
**Habit:** sleeps with gloves on. Takes them off to work. Puts them back on for sleep. The gloves are too large.
**Debt:** accepted payment from a Veilbreaker cell to smuggle a data chip off Morvos. Delivered the chip. The data is still on it. The copy in their bunk is insurance.

**Genetic:** dwarfism — proportionate: short. Built for mine shafts. Mammona recruited them for the height. Or the lack of it. Gets to places nobody else fits. Gets overlooked in other ways.

solid repair. Will hold. Properly done, properly sealed.

Cole wants to reach Automaton Processing Block before anyone else does. Won't say why. Offers everything he has.

**Gender:** Male | **Age:** 25 | **Occupation:** animal handler
**Traits:** Nimble, Low Stamina

**Tone:** cult_devotion

**Stats:**
Mining: 3 | Building: 7 | Cooking: 8 | Hunting: 4 | Research: 5 | Medical: 6
STR: 7 | END: 5 | AGI: 10 | PER: 5 | INT: 5 | CHA: 6 | WIL: 4 | EMP: 4

**Economic:**
Credits: 2,376 | Monthly: 1,576 (after Mammona deductions: ~267)
manages the budget. Sends money home when the comms work. Has a small reserve for emergencies. The reserve shrinks every month.

**Identity:**
compact and precise. Every movement economical. Trained or military — the efficiency doesn't come naturally. freckled and sun-damaged from a desert posting. The cold hasn't undone it. family runs a business on Paxtera Prime. He send money home. The family sends guilt back.

**Motivation:** find the person who sold them to Mammona and make it right. 'Right' is flexible (hidden)
**Hidden Agenda:** None apparent
**Social Mask:** Presents as the helper. Actually: doesn't know what they're worth outside of usefulness. If they stop helping, they stop existing. Tell: can't accept help. Deflects compliments. Works through injuries. The helping never stops because stopping means looking inward.

**Background:**
Scarshelf took twelve years from Adisa. Twelve years as a engineer. Left with debts, a bad knee, and a reference letter that damned with faint praise.

He was in cryo for longer than the contract specified. When he woke up, everyone he knew was dead.

Sleep comes in bursts. Two hours here, three there. Adisa has given up on a full cycle. Gets more done than anyone on the shift.

Adisa's transfer wasn't random. He bribed a Mammona clerk to get assigned here. The clerk is dead now.

The newcomer asked a question. The silence that followed wasn't angry. It was pitying.

**What Drives Them:** numbers. Counts everything. Steps, heartbeats, seconds between generator cycles. Finds patterns nobody else sees. Some of the patterns are real.
**What Haunts Them:** the recycled air. The taste of it. What he's breathing that the filters don't catch.

**Dialogue:**
- "Fresh contract? Yeah, I can tell by the eyes."
- "Check the dosimeter before going below level three. If it spikes, you've already been too long."
- "I came here to find something. Not thermal cores. Something else. Something personal."
- "Morale's been dropping since Tuesday. I checked -- nothing happened Tuesday. Nobody can explain it."
- "The medic's running tests she wasn't ordered to run."
- "The bunk mattresses are older than the colony. I know because they have a different colony's name stamped on them."

**Connections:**
**Connection:** widowed by Sasha Choi
**Love:** widowed. Doesn't wear the ring. Keeps it in a pocket. Takes it out when alone.

**Physical:** shrapnel scars across the back. Never had them removed. Says he's a reminder. Won't say of what.
**Habit:** keeps a list of everyone who's left the colony since they arrived. Names, dates, destinations. Thirty-seven names.
**Debt:** embezzled M-Points by exploiting a payroll glitch. Mammona fixed the glitch. They haven't fixed the thief.

**Sample Check:** Cooking (8) vs easy: rolled 26/95 -- strong success
made it work. The environment tried to kill them. They adapted faster.

**Quest Hook:**
Adisa has a data chip that proves Mammona knew about the bore shaft anomaly before the colony was placed. The chip has a kill-switch.

## NPC: Maren Vance
**Gender:** Female | **Age:** 54 | **Occupation:** blaster
**Traits:** Enduring, Nihilist
**Faction:** Dustweaver Swarm | **Archetype:** broken
**Tone:** cult_devotion

**Stats:**
Mining: 5 | Building: 4 | Cooking: 10 | Hunting: 2 | Research: 2 | Medical: 4
STR: 5 | END: 8 | AGI: 2 | PER: 4 | INT: 6 | CHA: 5 | WIL: 3 | EMP: 7

**Economic:**
Credits: 15,810 | Monthly: 2,802 (after Mammona deductions: ~476)
takes a percentage off the colony supply chain. Small amounts. Consistent. The kind of theft that works because nobody counts carefully enough. Mammona counts carefully. They just haven't counted here. Yet.

**Identity:**
average in every measurable way. The kind of person security cameras don't remember. compact build, low center of gravity. Stable on ice. Stable in a fight. mother died in a Mammona facility. Official cause: equipment failure. Unofficial cause: budget cuts. Carries the incident report in her bag.

**Motivation:** earn enough to pay off the debt and leave
**Hidden Agenda:** None apparent
**Social Mask:** Presents as the cynic. Actually: desperately hopeful. The cynicism is armor around something tender. Tell: reacts to good news with a flash of something unguarded before the cynicism catches up.

**Background:**
Ore freighters leaving Ashlock. That's Maren's earliest memory. The rumble through the floor of the hab unit. she wanted to be on one. Got her wish.

Maren was on shift when the containment failed. The colleague next to her didn't make it. Maren was wearing her gear.

Does her job and half of someone else's. Not because she's asked. Because the someone else can't.

The blood test results Mammona ran on Maren came back wrong. Not sick -- wrong. Different. They ran the test twice.

The ritual made no sense from outside. From inside, it was the only thing that made sense.

**What Drives Them:** radio. Scans frequencies every night. Mostly static. Sometimes something that might be music from very far away. Logs everything.
**What Haunts Them:** the cryo pod. Went in once. Remembers something from inside. Movement. Like something was in there with them.

**Dialogue:**
- "Welcome to the end of the line."
- "The thermal cores are warm. That's normal. If they're hot, drop it and run."
- "I haven't slept properly in weeks. Not insomnia. I hear things when I close my eyes. Coordinates. Repeated."
- "Something's changed. Can't point to it. The corridors feel different. Like the building shifted in the night."
- "Quartermaster's been marking inventory down. Not up. Down. Someone's skimming."
- "Don't let me become what this place makes people. I can feel it. The edges going soft. The caring getting expensive."
- "If anyone's listening. I don't need much. Just one more day."

**Connections:**
**Connection:** drinking buddy of Wynn Ivanov
**Love:** has a name tattooed on her ribs. Covered it with another tattoo. The first name is still visible if you look.

**Physical:** crooked nose. Broken at least three times. Set by hand each time. Has a story for each break.
**Habit:** practices card tricks with a deck missing three cards. Works around the gaps.
**Debt:** forged a medical license to get off Nerthus-9. The patients didn't know.

**Health:** strep — recurring: throat raw every few weeks. Voice drops to a rasp. The colony doesn't have the antibiotics to kill it properly, just enough to beat it back.
**Mental Health:** paranoia — worsening — started with reasonable suspicion. Mammona lies. That's a fact. But now it's everyone. The medic, the cook, the bunkmate. The network of deceit gets bigger every week. The worst part: some of it's probably true. verification. Tests. Small lies told to specific people to see where they surface. The testing is getting more elaborate.

**Sample Check:** Cooking (10) vs hard: rolled 79/85 -- success
survived. Hungry, cold, tired. But alive. That's the only metric that counts.

**Quest Hook:**
Maren found a Void Serpent contact frequency written on a smokestick wrapper in a place it shouldn't be. Now she can't stop dreaming about where it came from.

**Stats:**
Mining: 10 | Building: 8 | Cooking: 4 | Hunting: 2 | Research: 7 | Medical: 7
STR: 3 | END: 3 | AGI: 7 | PER: 7 | INT: 5 | CHA: 7 | WIL: 5 | EMP: 5

**Economic:**
Credits: 25,096 | Monthly: 1,548 (after Mammona deductions: ~263)
money from before the posting. Investments, inheritance, or something they don't discuss. The wealth makes them a target. The wealth also makes them useful.

**Identity:**
broad-backed from decades of physical labor. Stands with feet apart, hands ready. Even at rest. long fingers. Surgeon's hands, his mother called them. Uses them for wiring instead. family runs a business on Paxtera Prime. He send money home. The family sends guilt back.

**Motivation:** keep someone else from making the same mistake. Mentoring as penance
**Hidden Agenda:** None apparent
**Social Mask:** Presents as the grieving one. Actually: the grief is real but also useful. People don't question the grieving. Don't look too closely. Tell: the grief is selective. Appears when convenient. Disappears when it would cost them.

**Background:**
Silas Rimgate signed a Mammona contract on Voidbloom Cavern because the recruiter bought him dinner first. First hot meal in a month. He signed before dessert.

Heard the hum for the first time on Voidbloom Cavern. Low. Constant. Inside the skull. Hasn't stopped since.

Silas writes letters that he never sends. Folds them into tight squares. The drawer under his bunk is full of tight squares.

There's a reason Silas requested Erebus specifically. He knows what's down there. He wants to see it.

They played cards with a deck from the supply drop. Same brand as the ones on the Kennedy.

**What Drives Them:** tattoos. Does them by hand with a needle and salvaged ink. Each one a story. Each story has to be true. That's the rule.
**What Haunts Them:** the dark behind the walls. The sounds in there. Not mechanical. Wet. Shifting.

**Dialogue:**
- "Welcome to the end of the line."
- "If you end up on solo perimeter watch, keep talking. To yourself, to the radio, to nothing. Just keep talking."
- "I know what HERMES does at night. When we're all asleep. I checked the logs. I wish I hadn't."
- "Something's changed. Can't point to it. The corridors feel different. Like the building shifted in the night."
- "I heard Mammona's been here before. We're not the first team."
- "Let someone hear me. Just one person. That's all I need."

**Connections:**
**Connection:** protege of Pax Okafor
**Love:** sends birthday gifts through the supply chain. Takes three months to arrive. Sends them four months early. The math is careful.

**Physical:** pupils that dilate at different rates. Medical says it's fine. Nobody believes medical.
**Habit:** sings very quietly when alone. Voice carries three rooms. Denies it if asked. The songs are in a language nobody on the colony speaks.
**Debt:** owes a life debt to someone who died before it could be repaid

**Sample Check:** Mining (10) vs easy: rolled 85/95 -- success
quota met. Nothing special. Nothing wrong. That counts as a good day.

**Quest Hook:**
Silas intercepted a coded transmission between the colony and an unknown receiver. The encryption matches MasTema protocols.

## NPC: Nika Varga
**Gender:** Female | **Age:** 50 | **Occupation:** radio operator
**Traits:** Empathetic, Stubborn, Identity Stolen
**Faction:** Salt Column | **Archetype:** idealist
**Tone:** frontier_grit

**Stats:**
Mining: 4 | Building: 5 | Cooking: 8 | Hunting: 5 | Research: 10 | Medical: 6
STR: 4 | END: 6 | AGI: 4 | PER: 6 | INT: 5 | CHA: 7 | WIL: 4 | EMP: 7

**Economic:**
Credits: -42,730 | Monthly: 2,234 (after Mammona deductions: ~379)
owes more than they'll earn in the contract. Every paycheck goes to Mammona before it reaches them. Works for room and board. The room is a bunk. The board is NutriLoaf.

**Identity:**
lanky and awkward on flat ground. Climbs like something that evolved for vertical surfaces. a jawline sharp enough to cast shadows. Looks like they were carved from something harder than bone. has a younger sibling who idolizes them. The sibling doesn't know what they've done to keep them safe.

**Motivation:** find passage to the inner rim. Mammona's transit system is the only way. Mammona's transit system requires leverage (hidden)
**Hidden Agenda:** None apparent
**Social Mask:** Presents as the devout. Actually: uses faith as a framework for control. Belief optional. Tell: the prayers are public. The private hours are spent on something else entirely.

**Background:**
A deep diver by training, Nika ended up on Erebus because Salt Column needed someone expendable and she needed to disappear.

Nika survived HERMES started addressing people by names from a previous posting. Technically. The part that used to laugh didn't make it.

Sends half of every pay cycle to Palelock. Won't say who it goes to. The other half goes to the debt.

Nika has been dosing herself with something from the medical stores. Not for pain. For prevention. Won't say against what.

The drill bit broke again. She welded it with a torch made from a reactor coupling and a prayer.

**What Drives Them:** geology. Reads the rock like other people read faces. Runs her fingers over core samples with her eyes closed. Knows what's down there.
**What Haunts Them:** the supply ship not coming. Runs the numbers every day. Days of food. Days of fuel. The numbers are diverging.

**Dialogue:**
- "Keep your head down the first week. After that, keep it down permanently."
- "Ice is thinner near the east ridge. Walk soft or not at all."
- "I lied on my psych evaluation. Checked all the right boxes. Drew the clock face correctly. I'm not fine."
- "NutriLoaf batch forty-seven tastes different. Not worse. Different. Like pennies. Nobody can name how."
- "Quartermaster's been marking inventory down. Not up. Down. Someone's skimming."
- "I know where you sleep."
- "Guy on my last crew said he could fix anything. Reactor, drill, comms array. Couldn't fix his marriage though. Or the hull breach that killed him."

**Connections:**
**Connection:** widowed by Isa Varga
**Love:** promised to come back for someone. The promise is three years old. The shuttle costs haven't changed.

**Physical:** hands that shake unless occupied with work. Steadiest hands on the colony when she's holding a tool.
**Habit:** only speaks in the morning. Goes silent after noon. Nobody knows why. Responds to written notes after noon.
**Debt:** signed a Mammona non-disclosure agreement about something they saw in the deep bore on a previous posting. The NDA covers everything. Everything.

**Health:** hernia — inguinal: lifts wrong and pays for it. Should have had surgery before the posting. Mammona's pre-deployment medical is a checkbox, not an examination.

**Sample Check:** Research (10) vs easy: rolled 20/95 -- strong success
clean results. Reproducible. The kind of data that survives peer review.

**Quest Hook:**
The comms relay is broadcasting a repeating pattern. Nika recognized it. It's a lullaby her mother used to sing.

## NPC: Cassia Dvorak
**Gender:** Female | **Age:** 45 | **Occupation:** debt enforcer
**Traits:** Quick Thinker, Smokestick Habit, Thermal Reader
**Faction:** NexLink Communications
**Tone:** hollow_joy

**Stats:**
Mining: 2 | Building: 6 | Cooking: 10 | Hunting: 5 | Research: 6 | Medical: 4
STR: 4 | END: 5 | AGI: 3 | PER: 6 | INT: 7 | CHA: 4 | WIL: 4 | EMP: 5

**Economic:**
Credits: 1,704 | No salary — subsistence
earns enough to survive. Nothing left over. Can't afford passage off-planet. Can't afford to get sick. Can't afford anything that isn't survival.

**Identity:**
dense and low to the ground. Legs like pit props. Doesn't get knocked down. Doesn't get pushed back. family resemblance to someone in the colony records. Someone from a previous posting. The resemblance is exact. only child. Parents dead. No emergency contact on the Mammona form. Left it blank.

**Motivation:** protect the people they care about on the colony
**Hidden Agenda:** None apparent
**Social Mask:** Presents as the stoic. Actually: feeling everything. The discipline required to contain it is consuming them. Tell: jaw muscles. Hands clenched under tables. Eyes that track too carefully. The stillness is effort, not calm.

**Background:**
Survived a Mammona supply ship disappeared in the Erebus system on Crestkeep. Mammona covered the medical bills. Mammona always collects.

a chemical spill in the processing bay took her hearing in the left ear. Now she hears things nobody else does.

Cassia apologizes to machines before shutting them down. Quietly. As if the machines would mind. It drives the bunkmates crazy. Nobody says anything because of what happened last time someone did.

MasTema has a file on Cassia. Thin. But it exists. And someone keeps adding to it.

Celebration. Mandatory. The memo said 'morale event.' The attendees said nothing.

the bore drills run at 114 decibels. Mammona-issue ear protection is rated to 95. The difference is permanent hearing damage. Started with the high frequencies — couldn't hear the alarm tones. Now it's the mid-range. Conversations sound muffled, like everyone's talking through a wall. She are. The wall is scar tissue on the cochlea.

**What Drives Them:** reading. Has one book. Has read it forty times. Could recite it blind. Different passages mean different things each time.
**What Haunts Them:** losing her mind. Forgetting names. Getting confused about which shift it's. It's started. She keep a list.

**Dialogue:**
- "New face. I used to get excited about that. Now I just wonder how long you'll last."
- "Don't open sealed doors without checking the pressure gauge first. Lost a guy that way."
- "The day before the shuttle, I almost didn't get on. Stood at the boarding ramp for four minutes. Should've walked away."
- "Morale's been dropping since Tuesday. I checked -- nothing happened Tuesday. Nobody can explain it."
- "I heard Mammona's been here before. We're not the first team."
- "You didn't see anything. We're clear on that."
- "I asked HERMES for a weather report. It said 'cold.' Three syllables. Tax credits well spent."

**Connections:**
**Connection:** partner of Damian Petrov
**Love:** ended something to take this contract. Clean break. Professional. Still wakes up reaching for the other side of the bunk.

**Physical:** hands too large for her frame. Strong. Old scars on every knuckle. Gentle with small things.
**Habit:** apologizes to machines before shutting them down. Quietly. As if the machines would mind.
**Debt:** co-signed someone else's Mammona contract. That person died. The debt transferred.

**Sample Check:** Cooking (10) vs hard: rolled 21/85 -- strong success
made it work. The environment tried to kill them. They adapted faster.

**Quest Hook:**
Cassia found writing on the inside of a sealed pipe. Same handwriting as her own. She doesn't remember writing it.

**Gender:** Female | **Age:** 43 | **Occupation:** timberer

**Faction:** Order of the Hollow Signal | **Archetype:** rebel
**Tone:** resigned

**Stats:**
Mining: 2 | Building: 3 | Cooking: 2 | Hunting: 3 | Research: 3 | Medical: 8
STR: 2 | END: 6 | AGI: 8 | PER: 4 | INT: 3 | CHA: 6 | WIL: 8 | EMP: 6

**Economic:**
Credits: 179 | Monthly: 2,542 (after Mammona deductions: ~432)
arrived with nothing. Owns what Mammona issued: one uniform, one blanket, one set of utensils. Everything else is borrowed or stolen.

**Identity:**
heavy. Solid. The kind of weight that's half muscle and half refusal to care what anyone thinks. voice pitched low enough to vibrate through walls. Good for shouting orders. Bad for secrets. born during a colony evacuation. Birth certificate lists no planet. Just 'in transit.'.

**Motivation:** keep their head down and avoid being noticed by anyone with authority
**Hidden Agenda:** None apparent
**Social Mask:** Presents as the stoic. Actually: feeling everything. The discipline required to contain it is consuming them. Tell: jaw muscles. Hands clenched under tables. Eyes that track too carefully. The stillness is effort, not calm.

**Background:**
Raised by a seismic listener on Surface Rig Nerthus-7, Kaida can strip a generator blindfolded but never learned to read until age nineteen.

The scar on her sternum came from a skinwalker encounter on the perimeter. She tells a different story every time.

Knows every corridor, every vent, every locked door in the colony. Mapped them in the first week.

Under a false panel in her bunk, Kaida keeps a sealed drive containing evidence that the thing that sleeps beneath Erebus knows your name.

Nobody files complaints anymore.

joint pain that migrates. Wrists today, knees tomorrow. The fatigue comes in waves. Plans around the waves. Doesn't explain.

**What Drives Them:** medicine. Not licensed. Better than anyone licensed. Learned by watching someone die and deciding never again.
**What Haunts Them:** a specific date. Eight months from now. Won't say why. Gets quieter as it approaches.

**Dialogue:**
- "Another warm body. Good. We're short-handed."
- "You hear singing, you come back. Don't follow it."
- "I've been stealing rations. Not much. Enough to notice if someone counted. Nobody counts."
- "Two people on my shift have the same dream. They compared notes. Identical. Down to the color of the walls."
- "Two colonists were talking about the bore shaft at 0200. When I got closer, they stopped. Not like they paused -- like someone hit mute."
- "Don't let me become what this place makes people. I can feel it. The edges going soft. The caring getting expensive."
- "The medic told me to eat better. On Erebus. Eat better. With what? I'm not eating the walls. Yet."

**Connections:**
**Connection:** ward of Briar da Silva
**Love:** doesn't believe in love. Says it's chemical. Brain chemistry and proximity. Says it a lot. Mostly to themselves.

**Physical:** shrapnel scars across the back. Never had them removed. Says she's a reminder. Won't say of what.
**Habit:** keeps dried flowers in a tin. They've been dead for years. Arranges them differently each week.
**Debt:** gambled away their shuttle ticket on Karnaith

**Health:** core burn (thermal core extraction injury): thermal core extraction without proper shielding. The cores emit a radiation spectrum that standard gear doesn't block. The burns present as deep tissue damage — skin looks intact on the surface, but underneath the cells are dying. The tissue changes. Doesn't heal normally. Grows back different — thicker, darker, with a texture that's not quite skin. The extraction teams call it core burn. The scar tissue is warm to the touch. Always warm. Even on Erebus.

**Sample Check:** Medical (8) vs normal: rolled 92/80 -- failure
missed something. The symptom was subtle. The consequence won't be.

**Quest Hook:**
Kaida's shift partner vanished during a routine bore shaft check. The equipment came back. The person didn't.

### [SEQ:293] [History Event] [21:11:16]
============================================================

## HISTORY: HERMES Welcome Event
**Era:** fortuna | **Tone:** numb

The drill hit something. They noted it and kept drilling.

In 2565, Burnt Protocol conducted an unscheduled survey of Bore Shaft Seven. The team spent 2 weeks on site. The report they filed was 53 pages long. The redacted version is 11 pages.

**Significance:** This wasn't documented. Undocumented events on a Mammona posting aren't oversights. They're policy.

**Mechanical Impact:**
- Colony morale: -12 for until resolved (the news spreads)
- Section E: sealed (inaccessible until quest completes)
- NPC Tariq Wallace: arc shifts to 'compromised'
- HERMES begins logging increased activity in the area. The logs are not shared.

**Evidence:**
- Maintenance log #3361 (references an equipment requisition for items that don't exist)
- Residue on the ventilation grate -- organic, unidentified, warm to the touch
- A letter addressed to no one. The handwriting matches two different people.
- HERMES has the footage. HERMES won't share the footage.

**Who Knows:**
- **Adaeze Ivanov:** filed a report afterward. The report was rejected. They filed it again. Rejected again.
- **Nikolai Mbeki:** heard about it secondhand. Has theories. The theories are wrong but close.
- **Dante Norris:** arrived the week after. Wasn't briefed. But the bunk they were assigned to still smells like smoke.

**Aftermath:**
- A memorial was proposed by the colonists. Mammona denied it. The denial cited 'insufficient evidence of loss.'
- Burnt Protocol sent an investigator. The investigator stayed for three days, spoke to no one, and left.
- The night shift in the affected area has had four different crews in two months. Nobody asks for a second rotation.

**HERMES Analysis:**
HERMES's response to queries about this event changed on Day 43. Before: detailed, factual, cooperative. After: 'Please direct inquiries to your site manager.' The site manager was not informed of this change.

**Related Incidents:**
Three postings. Three events. Three sealed reports. The reports use the same classification code. The code isn't in any manual.

**Investigation Check:**
- Perception (8+, extreme) -- dig deeper into what happened
- **If successful:** the missing piece falls into place. Not an accident. Not an anomaly. A sequence.
- **If failed:** the trail goes cold. Not because the evidence is gone. Because someone cleaned it. Recently.

============================================================

## HISTORY: The Missing Report
**Era:** corporate | **Tone:** psychic_contamination

The dreams were sequential now. Each night picked up where the last one ended.

The thermal core readings at Edge of Oblivion spike every 69 hours. The interval matches no known geological cycle. It does match the heart rate of something very large and very slow.

**Significance:** This pattern predates human presence on the planet. It continued through human presence on the planet. It's continuing now.

**Mechanical Impact:**
- Colony morale: -5 for two weeks (the news spreads)
- the cryogenics wing: sealed (inaccessible until quest completes)
- NPC Rashid Belov: arc shifts to 'cooperative'
- HERMES reclassifies the location. The new classification is above colony clearance.

**Evidence:**
- Maintenance log #5875 (references nothing. The entry for that date is blank. Every other entry is filled.)
- Tool marks on the sealed door -- someone tried to open it. From the inside.
- A letter addressed to no one. The handwriting matches two different people.
- HERMES flagged the event internally, then removed the flag. The removal is logged. The original flag is not.

**Who Knows:**
- **Kaida Huang:** was there. Won't talk about it. The silence is loud.
- **Sigrid Chen:** has been asking questions. The questions are making people uncomfortable.
- **Mirabel Blair:** has no connection to the event. But they've been dreaming about it. Accurately.

**Aftermath:**
- Shift schedules were restructured. The restructuring moved three people away from the affected area. No reason given.
- A sealed communication was sent to MasTema. Response: 'Acknowledged.' No follow-up. No action. Just acknowledgment.
- Three colonists requested bunk transfers away from the affected area. Two were approved. The third withdrew the request without explanation.

**HERMES Analysis:**
HERMES classification: EVENT-732. Status: ARCHIVED. Probability of recurrence: 33%. Recommended action: NONE. This recommendation has not been reviewed by any human authority.

**Related Incidents:**
Three postings. Three events. Three sealed reports. The reports use the same classification code. The code isn't in any manual.

**Investigation Check:**

Water damage on the wall was forming shapes. More shapes every day.

In 2565, The Silent Reef conducted an unscheduled survey of Colony Base Camp. The team spent 3 weeks on site. The report they filed was 189 pages long. The redacted version is 9 pages.

**Significance:** Mammona's records don't match reality. That's either incompetence or intent. Mammona isn't incompetent.

- Residue on the ventilation grate -- organic, unidentified, warm to the touch

- **Sasha Choi:** has no connection to the event. But they've been dreaming about it. Accurately.

- The Ashen Circuit made inquiries. The inquiries were careful. Indirect. The kind of careful that means they already know.

HERMES has cross-referenced this event with 7 similar incidents across 3 postings. The correlation is strong. HERMES has not shared this correlation with colony staff. When queried: 'Insufficient clearance.'

### [SEQ:296] [History Event] [21:11:17]

## HISTORY: Thermal Anomaly Cycle
**Era:** present | **Tone:** survival_horror

Two medkits for seventeen people. The math was simple. The choices weren't.

Fourteen crates arrived on the last supply drop before the previous posting ended. The manifest lists medical supplies. The crates weigh three times what medical supplies weigh. They were moved to Section B under guard. The lock code was changed after delivery.

**Significance:** The timeline doesn't add up. Something is missing, and the gap is shaped like a decision somebody made.

**Mechanical Impact:**
- Colony morale: -10 for until resolved (the news spreads)
- the lower levels: sealed (inaccessible until quest completes)
- NPC Ash Dvorak: arc shifts to 'paranoid'
- HERMES begins logging increased activity in the area. The logs are not shared.

**Evidence:**
- Maintenance log #1042 (references the incident obliquely -- 'scheduled cleaning')
- Residue on the ventilation grate -- organic, unidentified, warm to the touch
- A sketch of the bore shaft showing a passage that isn't on any schematic. The sketch is accurate.
- HERMES's sensor data shows a temperature spike in the affected area. Duration: 0.3 seconds. Magnitude: +200C.

**Who Knows:**
- **Colette Ashford:** was the first to arrive at the scene. What they saw doesn't match the official account.
- **Duke Vasquez:** heard about it secondhand. Has theories. The theories are wrong but close.
- **Lev Caro:** has no connection to the event. But they've been dreaming about it. Accurately.

**Aftermath:**
- Colony protocol was updated. The update references the event obliquely. Nobody asked for the update.
- A sealed communication was sent to MasTema. Response: 'Acknowledged.' No follow-up. No action. Just acknowledgment.
- Three colonists requested bunk transfers away from the affected area. Two were approved. The third withdrew the request without explanation.

**HERMES Analysis:**
HERMES maintains a shadow log of the event. The shadow log contains data that contradicts the official record. Both records are internally consistent. Both cannot be true.

**Related Incidents:**
An unrelated investigation at Twin Sun Observatory uncovered documentation referencing this event by date. The documentation predates the event by 8 years.

**Investigation Check:**
- Research (6+, hard) -- dig deeper into what happened
- **If successful:** the pattern becomes visible. This happened before. Under different names. Same outcome.
- **If failed:** dead end. But the dead end is instructive -- someone put a wall exactly where you needed a door.
